- **Given** Host control, **then** the question screen is unchanged and shows no Answers panel (regression check).
- *Dependencies / risks:* the server clock decides arrival order and lateness; a slow phone loses about one poll interval. Done when a full game can be played with every answer typed on phones.

### US-65 Phone buzzer: Golden Goal bets on phones (Should)
As a contestant, I want to place my Golden Goal bet privately on my phone, so that nobody in the room sees my bet before the answers. *(Added 2026-11-27, owner, D-80. Phone Sprint 3, branch `phone-buzzer`, with the release of the Phone buzzer. Size M. Builds on US-44 and US-64; adds the bets table to `sql/migrate-add-rooms.sql`.)*
- **Given** Phone buzzer mode and Golden Goal starts (US-44 rules, D-51), **then** the masked bet entry on the host is replaced by a **private bet screen on each phone** showing the category, the player's score and the allowed range; the host screen shows "Waiting for players to bet (n/N)" with each name marked bet or waiting, never the amounts.
- **Given** a bet, **then** it is a whole number from **0 up to the contestant's bet limit** (D-80 j: the D-48 limit, score or the largest enabled point value, whichever is higher, as in Host control; owner to confirm, FU-117). The server rejects a value outside it (400). A sent bet is locked (a second one → 409).
- **Given** a contestant has no joined device or loses it, **then** the host can tap **Continue without missing bets**; each missing bet counts as 0 (D-80 k). Release and rejoin also work during the wait.
- **When** all bets are in (or the host continues), **then** the Golden Goal question opens and follows the US-64 rules: question and text box on phones, answers in arrival order on the host, one locked answer, late answers rejected, the answer never sent to phones.
- **When** the host has marked everyone, **then** the bets and results are revealed on the host screen, scored as US-44 (Correct adds, Wrong subtracts the bet). A repeat round (still tied) asks for new bets on the phones the same way.
- **Given** a refresh of the host or a phone during the bet wait, **then** the bets already sent are kept and the wait resumes; a phone that had bet shows its bet locked.
- **Given** Host control, **then** Golden Goal is unchanged (masked entry one by one on the host; regression check). Tournament Golden Goal (including Mokh Bot) is unchanged.
- **Given** the game ends (Results, New game) or the room expires, **then** the phones show the game-over screen with the final standings, or "This game has ended."
- **Given** AR, **then** the bet and game-over screens are right to left with numbers isolated (D-60); the Arabic text gets a review before release.
- *Dependencies / risks:* Done when a live smoke test on Hostinger with 3 or more real phones passes and a full game including Golden Goal is played in Players and Teams mode.

### US-53 Kilo Meshakkal (كيلو مشكل) category (Should)
As a host, I want a "Kilo Meshakkal" category whose boxes each hide a question from a different random category and a mystery value, so that one column of the board is a surprise mix. *(Added 2026-11-03, owner request, design approved by the owner. Size M. Sprint 10, D-53. Accepted by the PO 2026-11-03 (D-54); BUG-10-01 fixed. Pending the owner's checks FU-36 and FU-37.)*
- **Given** at least one playable category, **when** setup loads, **then** a "Kilo Meshakkal" tile (its name only, no hint: owner change 2026-11-03 after acceptance) is in the category list, sorted by name with the other categories (under "K"). With no playable category it is not shown.
- **Given** the category list, **then** Kilo Meshakkal counts as one of the N selections (`categories_per_game`), gets a tick-order badge and is locked at N like any other tile. Ticked alone, it gives a 1-column board.
- **Given** "Surprise me!" is ticked, alone or with other picks, **then** it never picks Kilo Meshakkal; every hidden "?" column is a real category. Kilo Meshakkal + Surprise gives a Kilo Meshakkal column and 1 hidden real category.
- **Given** Start with Kilo Meshakkal ticked, **then** the board has a "Kilo Meshakkal" column in its tick-order position, with one box per enabled point value. Each box holds a random question from any playable category at that box's value. No question appears twice on the board (also not in the other columns). Questions not yet played on this device come first (US-30 played-list rule). The boxes come from different categories where possible: with at least as many playable categories as point values, every box has a different source category.
- **Given** `POST questions.php?board=1` with categories `-1`, **then** the column comes back with id `-1`, name "Kilo Meshakkal", one question per enabled point value, each with its source `category_name`. With `3,-1,1` the mix column is in the middle. `-1,-1` or `-2` → 400. When no question is left for a point value, → 400 with a readable message naming the value, and no board.
- **Given** the board, **then** each open Kilo Meshakkal box shows an emoji instead of its point value, each box a different emoji. The column holds every enabled point value exactly once, in a random order (not ascending), and the order and emoji change between games.
- **Given** a screen reader, **then** an open Kilo Meshakkal box is read as "Kilo Meshakkal, mystery value"; its label and tooltip never contain the points.
- **When** the host opens a Kilo Meshakkal box, **then** the question heading reads "Kilo Meshakkal: <source category> – <points>" (for example "Kilo Meshakkal: Football – 300"), and Correct / Wrong score that value.
- **Given** a used Kilo Meshakkal box, **then** it shows its point value with the owner's color and name, or grey if nobody was correct, like any other box.
- **Given** a refresh mid-game, **then** the same emoji, the same value order and the same headings come back. A saved Kilo Meshakkal column whose value order is not one of each board value, or whose emoji list is missing or the wrong length, is treated as corrupt (Setup shown, US-17). Saves from before this story still load.
- **Given** a Kilo Meshakkal box, **then** Swap, Skip, 3 choices, Report (the report names the question's real category), the double-score cell (D-40) and Golden Goal (US-44) work as on any box.
- **Given** a game that includes Kilo Meshakkal, **then** the game log line lists it as "Kilo Meshakkal" among the categories. `game-log.php` with `[-1, 3]` → 201; `-1` twice or any other id below 1 → 400.
- **Given** Admin, **then** Kilo Meshakkal is not listed in Categories, not offered in the Questions category filter or form, and cannot be renamed, deleted or given questions. It has no row in the `categories` table, and it does not show in the played-questions progress.
- **Given** the board and setup at 1366x768 and 375x812, **then** they still fit as D-30 says, with the emoji sized like a point label. Source category names are shown as text, never as HTML.
- **Given** the guide, **then** `how-to-play.html` has one bullet on Kilo Meshakkal: questions from any category, values hidden behind emoji.
- *Dependencies / risks:* no DB migration; reserved id `-1` on server and client (Surprise stays `0`). The emoji are a deliberate product choice (D-53), so the ux-ui plugin's no-emoji check records an exception instead of a fix. A small bank can force two boxes from the same category (allowed, "where possible").

### US-54 Free second chance in the Streak Challenge (Should)
As a streak player, I want one second chance per run, so that a single slip doesn't end a long streak. *(Added 2026-11-11, owner request, D-65. Size S. Client-only: no API or database change. Streak Challenge only; US-51, the rewarded-ad version, stays planned.)*
- **Given** a Streak Challenge run that has not used its second chance, **when** the player picks a wrong answer or the time runs out, **then** the usual feedback shows (pick red, right answer green, "Time's up!" if late), the run is not over, and a **"Second chance!" popup** (the double-score popup's card) opens with the feedback, **Use second chance** (primary) and **See results**. The popup must be answered: Escape does nothing and Android Back means See results.
- **When** the player clicks **Use second chance** (or presses Enter while it is focused), **then** the streak count is unchanged, the missed question is replaced by a new question the run has not shown (fresh timer), and the chance is used up for the rest of the run.
- **When** the player clicks **See results** (or Android Back), **then** the run ends as before and the streak is saved.
- **Given** a run that used its second chance, **when** the player misses again, **then** the run ends as before (no offer).
- **Given** a refresh while the choice is open, **then** the same choice is offered again; a refresh after using the chance keeps it used. A saved run from before this change loads and counts as unused.
- **Given** the next question cannot be loaded, **then** the chance is not used up and the player can try again or choose See results.
- **Given** the Integration Quiz and the Daily Challenge, **then** they are unchanged (one miss ends the run or the day's question set continues as before).
- **Given** the leaderboard, **then** it shows no marker for runs that used the chance (D-65).
- **Given** the setup and question hints, the guide (`how-to-play.html`) and `llms.txt`, **then** they mention the one second chance, in English and Arabic.
- *Dependencies / risks:* none. Later: design how a player earns the chance (D-65). Everyone gets it free for now. *(2026-11-16: proposed as US-57, D-69. 2026-11-17: the owner set the rule (FU-82 closed) and US-57 replaces the free chance: a run starts with 0 and earns its chances, D-69.)*

### US-56 Challenge a friend (Should) — CANCELLED 2026-11-18
As a streak player, I want to send a friend a link to the same questions I just played, so that we can see who gets the longer streak. *(Added 2026-11-16, owner request. Size M. **Cancelled 2026-11-18 by the owner** (FU-80 and FU-81 cancelled with it); nothing was built. D-68 is kept as the record. US-55 is used on the unmerged `christmas-theme` branch.)*

*Creating a challenge*
- **Given** a Streak Challenge run that ended with a streak of 1 or more, **then** the leaderboard screen shows "Challenge a friend" next to "Play again". It is not shown for a streak of 0, nor on the leaderboard opened from the setup (no run).
- **When** the player clicks "Challenge a friend", **then** `POST api/challenges.php` stores the challenge and the existing Share flow (US-46, D-47 d) opens: the share sheet on a touch device, otherwise the text is copied and "Copied" shows. The text reads "Can you beat my 14 in a row on Mokh Rush?" plus the link `https://brainrush.integration.ninja/?challenge=<id>`.
- **Given** the link, **then** it holds only a random challenge id (at least 12 URL-safe characters from a secure random source, not a sequence number), never question ids, answers or the name. The server row holds the id, the challenger name, the streak, the timer in seconds, the ordered ids of every question the run showed (missed ones and second-chance replacements included) and the creation time. No IP, device id or friend data is stored (rate limit: FU-81).
- **Given** the challenger's run, **then** it is saved on the leaderboard as today (US-34); creating a challenge adds no leaderboard row and changes none.

*Opening a challenge*
- **When** someone opens a valid link with no game or run in progress, **then** the Streak setup opens in challenge mode, in any Players/Teams state: a banner "Sara challenges you: beat 14 in a row!", the "Challenger name" field (pre-filled as today), the timer shown as "Timer: 30 s (set by the challenge)" and not editable, and "Start challenge". "Back to a normal game" drops the challenge and removes `?challenge=` from the address.
- **Given** a board game or Streak run in progress on the device, **when** the link is opened, **then** that game or run is restored as today (nothing is discarded) and a notice says "Sara's challenge is waiting: open the link again after this game."
- **Given** an unknown or expired id (older than 30 days, FU-81), **then** "This challenge has expired." shows on the normal Streak setup. A malformed id gives the same message; the API answers 404.
- **Given** a challenge whose questions were deleted, or whose point value was disabled, since it was created, **then** those questions are skipped and the rest are played in order, with the note "1 question from this challenge is no longer available and was skipped." If none is left, it is treated as expired.

*Playing it*
- **Given** a challenge run, **then** the friend gets the challenger's questions in the same order, with the challenger's timer, no helpers (D-37 d) and the current second-chance rule (US-54, or US-57 once built). A second chance moves to the next question in the list. The choices are built as in any Streak run, so their order (and borrowed wrong answers) can differ from the challenger's (accepted, as D-47 g).
- **Given** the friend has answered every question in the list, **then** the run goes on with normal random Streak questions (unseen first, the list excluded) until a miss or "End run".
- **Given** a refresh mid-run, **then** the run comes back with its challenge (id, name, streak to beat, list position) from `brainrush.streak` (optional field, no version bump, as D-56 a). An invalid challenge field discards the run like any invalid save (US-33).

*Result*
- **Given** the challenge run ends, **then** the leaderboard screen shows, above the rank line: "You beat Sara's 14!" (higher), "Level with Sara: 14 each." (equal) or "Sara still leads: 14 vs 9." (lower, including 0).
- **Given** the friend's run, **then** it is saved on the leaderboard as a normal run under the US-34 rules (PO proposal, owner to confirm, FU-80). A streak of 0 is not saved, as today.
- **Given** the result, **then** "Challenge a friend" is offered for the friend's own run (streak 1 or more), creating a new challenge from it.

*Language, app, API, abuse*
- **Given** AR, **then** the banner, notices, result lines, share text and errors show in Arabic, right to left, with names and numbers isolated (D-58 c, D-60); the strings are AI-drafted pending native review (D-58 a).
- **Given** the Android app (US-40) installed, **when** the link is opened on the phone, **then** it opens in the app if Android routes the site link there, otherwise in Chrome; the challenge works the same in both. Android Back on an open challenge question behaves as on a Streak question.
- **Given** `POST api/challenges.php` `{name, streak, timer, question_ids}` (public, JSON only), **then** the name follows the streaks rules (control characters become spaces, 1–30 characters), the streak is a whole number from 1 to 100000, the timer is within the timer limits of the setup, and `question_ids` is a list of 1 to 500 distinct whole numbers with at least `streak` entries; otherwise 400. Valid → 201 `{id}`. Over the rate limit → 429 "Too many challenges. Try again later." (limit FU-81). Expired rows are deleted during a POST (no cron).
- **Given** `GET api/challenges.php?id=<id>`, **then** 200 `{name, streak, timer, questions, skipped}` with questions in the Streak batch format, or 404. Prepared statements only (US-26); names are shown as text, never HTML (US-27).
- **Given** the guide, `privacy.html` (what a challenge stores and for how long) and `llms.txt`, **then** they describe the feature.
- *Dependencies / risks:* needs a new `challenges` table (`sql/migrate-add-challenges.sql` before the code, both schema files) and a new API, `api/challenges.php`. The GET returns the answers, like every Streak batch, so a determined user can read them (accepted, D-37 g); a challenger can also replay their own challenge knowing the answers (see FU-80). Whether the TWA opens the link in the app depends on the app's link handling: check it on a device. Rate limiting without storing IPs is an owner choice (FU-81). Must use the same second-chance rule as US-57 for both players.

### US-57 Earn the second chance (Should)
As a streak player, I want to earn my second chances by answering well, so that a long run is rewarded and the chance feels earned. *(Added 2026-11-16, owner request. Size S. Resolves D-65 f. Client-only: no API or database change. D-69. Rule set by the owner 2026-11-17 (FU-82 closed); replaces the PO's first proposal (start with 1, earn 1 every 10, cap 2). Built 2026-11-17 on branch `us-57-earn-chance`, dev note `docs/sprints/us-57-dev.md`. **PO accepted 2026-11-17** (test report `docs/sprints/us-57-test-report.md`, D-70), merged to `main` (PR #125). CR hearts (D-71) on branch `chance-hearts`: **PO accepted 2026-11-17**, awaiting the owner's local Chrome test (FU-83).)*
- **Given** a new Streak Challenge run, **then** it starts with **0** second chances (the free US-54 chance goes away).
- **Given** a run, **when** the player gets **5** correct answers, **then** the run earns its **1st** chance and the counter restarts; **10 more** correct answers earn the **2nd**. At most **2** chances are earned per run; after the 2nd the counter stops. Only correct answers count; a miss does not move the counter.
- **Given** a correct answer that earns a chance, **then** the feedback reads "Correct! Your streak is 5. +1 second chance!".
- **Given** a Streak run, from its first question, **then** the line under "Streak N" shows the counter "Next chance: 3/5" (then "Next chance: 0/10") and **2 heart slots**, like game lives: one **red** heart per chance held, the others **gray** (not earned yet, or used: both look the same). Red hearts fill from the start side (the right in Arabic). A fresh run shows 2 gray hearts; no "1 chance", "2 chances" or "No chances left" text appears anywhere on the line. The counter is hidden once both chances are earned; the hearts stay. The line fits 1366x768 and 375x812 in English and Arabic, and also with the Christmas theme on (US-55). The Integration Quiz and Daily Challenge show no hearts. *(Changed by the CR hearts, D-71; replaces the held count text of D-70 a.)*
- **Given** a correct answer that earns a chance, **then** the heart that turns red plays a short **pop** (about 0.5 s). **Given** **Use second chance**, **then** the heart that is emptied plays a **break** (crack, shrink, fade to gray, about 0.55 s). The animations play only on that live event: never on a refresh, Next, a language switch or the popup opening, and they cause no layout shift. With reduced motion turned on in the device settings, the heart only changes colour.
- **Given** a screen reader, **then** the hearts are read as one image "Second chances: 1 of 2" (current count, in the page language); the heart drawings themselves are not read, and a change on the line is announced once.
- **Given** a miss (wrong pick or time-out) while holding 1 or more chances, **then** the existing "Second chance!" popup opens (US-54) and says how many chances the player has. **Use second chance** takes 1 off, keeps the streak (never reset by a chance, as US-54) and **restarts the counter at 0**: after using the 1st chance, the 2nd needs 10 correct answers counted from that use. With 0 chances held, a miss ends the run as today (no popup).
- *Examples:* no misses: chances at streak 5 and 15 (holds 2). Earn at 5, miss and use at streak 8: counter 0/10, 2nd chance at streak 18.
- **Given** a refresh, **then** the streak, chances and counter are kept (optional `chances` field `{earned, used, progress, earnedAt}` in `brainrush.streak`, where `earnedAt` is the streak at the last earn and drives the "+1" line, no version bump, as D-56 a). Whole numbers with 0 <= used <= earned <= 2 and 0 <= progress < the next goal (0 once both are earned), else the run is discarded like any invalid save (US-33). A run saved under US-54 loads as if it had earned its free chance: unused -> 1 earned, 0 used; used -> 1 earned, 1 used; counter 0.
- **Given** the Integration Quiz and the Daily Challenge, **then** they are unchanged.
- **Given** the leaderboard, **then** it shows no marker for runs that used chances (D-65 d).
- **Given** a challenge run (US-56), **then** the friend plays with this same rule.
- **Given** the setup and question hints, the guide and `llms.txt`, **then** they describe the rule in English and Arabic instead of "one second chance" (Arabic AI-drafted pending native review, D-58 a; numbers isolated, D-58 c, D-60).
- *CR hearts (owner request 2026-11-17, D-71):* the held count text is replaced by the hearts in the three criteria above. Dev note `docs/sprints/us-57-dev.md` and test report `docs/sprints/us-57-test-report.md`, sections "CR hearts" (58 PASS / 0 FAIL). **PO accepted 2026-11-17.**
- *Dependencies / risks:* none; the 5, the 10 and the cap of 2 are named constants in one place (`CHANCE_FIRST_AT`, `CHANCE_NEXT_AFTER`, `CHANCE_MAX` in `js/game.js`). If US-56 ships later, it uses this rule for both players (D-69). US-51 (rewarded ad) stays a separate possible extra chance.

*Handled in other sessions, not stories here:*
- Seasonal packs: being done as the Christmas theme in another session (branch `christmas-theme`).
- Picture questions (e.g. footballer photos in Sports): the owner is starting them in another session.

## Epic E18 — Seasonal themes

### US-55 Christmas theme (Should)
As the owner, I want to switch a Christmas look and greeting on and off from Admin, so that players get a festive Mokh Rush in December without a redeploy, and the code is easy to remove afterwards. *(Added 2026-11-16, owner request, D-66. Size M. No migration: missing settings rows count as off. Game page (`index.html`) only. Branch `christmas-theme`. To be removed by a separate CR after Christmas.)*
- **Given** Admin > Game settings, **then** it has a checkbox "Christmas theme" (setting `christmas_theme`, 0/1) and a text field "Christmas greeting" (setting `christmas_banner_text`, max 150 characters), loaded and saved like `banner_enabled` / `banner_text`.
- **Given** a database with no `christmas_theme` or `christmas_banner_text` row, **then** `GET api/settings.php` returns `christmas_theme` 0 and an empty greeting, and the game page shows no Christmas theme.
- **Given** `PUT api/settings.php` with `christmas_theme` other than 0 or 1, or `christmas_banner_text` longer than 150 characters, **then** → 400 and nothing is saved; Admin shows the error. Exactly 150 characters → saved.
- **Given** the theme is off, **then** the game page looks and behaves as before this story: no `theme-christmas` class on `<body>`, no snow layer, no greeting strip, `<meta name="theme-color">` unchanged, no console errors.
- **Given** the theme is switched on in Admin, **when** the game page is reloaded, **then** `<body>` has the class `theme-christmas` and the page shows: a festive palette (deep pine background and panels, red accent, gold kept for point values and primary buttons), a string of lights under the header, a small Santa hat on the header logo, and a light snowfall layer behind the content (`aria-hidden="true"`, does not block clicks).
- **Given** the theme is on, **then** a greeting strip shows on the setup screen with the admin text; when the text is empty it shows the built-in default ("Merry Christmas from Mokh Rush!" in English, the Arabic equivalent in Arabic). The admin text is shown as text, never as HTML (US-27).
- **Given** the theme is on, **then** `<meta name="theme-color">` is set to the festive background colour.
- **Given** the theme is on and the player returns, **then** the theme is applied from the last known state before the settings call completes (no flash of the normal theme); if the setting has been switched off since, the theme is removed once the settings call returns.
- **Given** the OS setting `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, **then** the snow does not animate (or is not shown); the rest of the theme stays.
- **Given** the theme is on, **then** emojis may appear in the greeting strip, beside the header title and as board accents (D-66); decorative emojis have `aria-hidden="true"`.
- **Given** the theme is on, **then** the setup screen, board, question popup, helpers, Golden Goal, Daily Challenge and Streak Challenge screens are readable and usable (snow never covers text or controls in a way that hurts reading) at 1366x768, 1920x1080 and 375 px wide, in English and in Arabic (RTL).
- **Given** the theme is on, **then** text, point values and buttons meet WCAG AA contrast (4.5:1 normal text, 3:1 large text and UI parts) in the new palette.
- **Given** the other pages (Admin, guide, About, Contact, Privacy, Terms, Integration Quiz, offline page), **then** they are unchanged with the theme on.
- **Given** the code, **then** all theme styling is in `css/christmas.css` (scoped under `.theme-christmas`) and all game-side logic is in `js/christmas.js`; `js/game.*.js` and `css/style.*.css` are unchanged; every hook in `index.html`, `admin.html`, `js/admin.*.js` and `api/settings.php` carries a `CHRISTMAS` comment.
- **Given** the dev notes, **then** they include a removal checklist: files to delete, every marked hook to remove, and the one SQL line that deletes the two settings rows.
- *Dependencies / risks:* the ux-ui plugin's no-emoji check will flag the Christmas files and greeting by design (D-66, exception recorded, not a fix); it must stay clean everywhere else. The Android app picks the theme up from the website with no app release. Both new assets are content-hashed by the pre-commit hook.
- *PO acceptance (2026-11-16):* **Accepted with follow-ups** (test report `docs/sprints/cr-us55-christmas-test-report.md`, "PO acceptance"). 22 of 24 criteria PASS at test; C12 FAIL closed by fix loop 1 (BUG-55-02 hat at <=600 px moved to `top: -0.5rem`, BUG-55-03 `--border` `#5a9a72`, 3.52:1 on panel-2); BUG-55-01 (white on the default red player card, 3.91:1) is pre-existing in `js/game.*.js`, outside this CR, and moves to FU-75. Reduced motion, first-paint timing and the Python UX gates go to the owner's local check (FU-76). Wording: the endpoint is `PUT` only (POST is 405, OBS-55-01). Decisions D-67.

## Epic E19 — Daily Crossword

### US-58 Daily Crossword (Should)
As a player, I want a small daily crossword built from Mokh Rush questions, the same for everyone that day, so that I have a calm solo puzzle to come back to and compare times with friends. *(Added 2026-11-20, owner request, D-72. Size L. Branch `crossword`. Needs a migration `sql/migrate-add-daily-crossword.sql`, mirrored in both schema files, run before the code.)*

*Admin and entry*
- **Given** Admin > Game settings, **then** it has a checkbox "Daily Crossword" (setting `crossword_enabled`, 0/1), loaded and saved like `final_round_enabled`. `PUT api/settings.php` with any other value → 400 and nothing is saved. A database with no `crossword_enabled` row returns 0 (off), so no settings migration is needed.
- **Given** `crossword_enabled` is 0, **then** the game page shows no crossword button and looks as before this story. **Given** it is 1, **when** the game page is (re)loaded, **then** a "Daily Crossword" button (`#crossword-open-btn`) shows next to the Daily Challenge button, under the same rules (hidden in maintenance mode and in the Streak setup).
- **Given** the button is clicked, **then** `GET api/crossword.php` is fetched and the crossword screen (`#screen-crossword`) opens: today's puzzle, or the finished result if this device already solved it (see *End*). A "Back" button returns to the setup screen and keeps the progress.

*Words (server)*
- **Given** the question bank, **then** a word candidate comes only from a **text** question (no picture) at an **enabled** point value, in any category.
- **Given** an answer, **then** it is cleaned in this order: cut at the first " (" (as the 3-choices rule), drop a leading "The " (any case), remove spaces, hyphens and apostrophes, fold accents (é → E, ñ → N, ü → U), uppercase. Examples: "The Beatles" → `BEATLES`; "Spider-Man" → `SPIDERMAN`; "Pelé" → `PELE`; "Paris (France)" → `PARIS`; "O'Neal" → `ONEAL`.
- **Given** the cleaned word, **then** it is kept only if it matches `^[A-Z]{3,10}$`: "Ox" (2), "Constantinople" (14), "1969", "Apollo 11" and any word with other characters left are dropped.
- **Given** two questions that give the same word, **then** only one of them can be in a puzzle. **Given** a word that appears in its own clue (English or Arabic question text, case- and accent-insensitive substring), **then** that question is not used.
- **Given** a picture question, a disabled point value or a question with an empty answer, **then** it never appears in the crossword.

*Grid (server)*
- **Given** a UTC day (D-46) with no stored puzzle, **when** the first request arrives, **then** the server builds a grid, stores it in `daily_crossword` with `INSERT IGNORE` and reads it back; two simultaneous first requests get the same grid and only one row exists.
- **Given** two requests on the same UTC day (any device, any time zone), **then** they return the identical layout (same words, positions, directions and numbers). At midnight UTC a new puzzle is built.
- **Given** a stored grid, **then** it has **6 to 10 words**, fits in **13 × 13** cells at most (`width`, `height` ≤ 13, trimmed to the words), every word crosses at least one other (one connected grid), and every crossing cell has the same letter in both words.
- **Given** a stored grid, **then** no two words touch side by side (no two parallel words in adjacent rows or columns sharing a stretch), and the cell before the first letter and after the last letter of every word is empty (no word runs into another end to end). Only crossings join words.
- **Given** the bank cannot give a grid of at least 6 words, **then** `GET api/crossword.php` → **503** with the generic message (as Daily), no row is stored, and the game shows "The crossword isn't available right now. Try again later." on the setup screen. Without the `daily_crossword` table → 503 the same way. Other methods → 405.
- **Given** the response, **then** it has `date`, `number` (#N, D-72 n), `seconds_to_next`, `width`, `height` and `words[]` with `n`, `row`, `col`, `dir` (`across`/`down`), `length`, `answer`, `question`, `question_ar`, `category_name`, `category_name_ar`. The answers are in the payload (accepted, D-37 g).

*Play*
- **Given** the grid, **then** word cells are white with the clue number in the top-left corner of each word's first cell (standard numbering: left to right, top to bottom; a cell starting an across and a down word has one number); other cells are dark and cannot be selected.
- **Given** a selected cell, **when** the player types a letter on a keyboard or a **phone keyboard** (the on-screen keyboard opens on tap and stays open), **then** it is shown uppercase in the cell and the cursor moves to the next cell of the active word. Only A–Z are accepted (lowercase is uppercased, digits, symbols and Arabic letters are ignored).
- **Given** Backspace, **then** it clears the current cell, or if it is already empty moves back one cell in the active word and clears that one. **Given** an arrow key, **then** the cursor moves to the next word cell in that direction (skipping dark cells) and the direction follows the arrow's axis.
- **Given** a selected cell, **when** it is clicked or tapped again, **then** the direction switches between across and down (only if a word runs both ways there). The active word's cells are highlighted, the current cell more strongly.
- **Given** the clue panel, **then** it lists "Across" and "Down" clues as "number. clue text (length)", the active clue highlighted and scrolled into view; clicking a clue selects its word (first empty cell, else the first cell). On a phone (≤ 600 px) the active clue also shows in a bar above the grid. *(Amended at acceptance 2026-11-20, D-72 s: the bar shows above the grid at every width.)*
- **Given** a clue that has Arabic text, **then** an EN/AR toggle on the active clue switches that clue's language (as `toggleDailyLang`); a clue without Arabic shows no toggle. The starting language follows D-72 g.
- **Given** "Check word", **then** the wrong letters of the active word are marked (red) until they are changed; correct and empty cells are not marked. Check is free and unlimited.
- **Given** "Reveal letter", **then** the current cell gets the right letter, is locked (typing skips it) and is shown as revealed; the hints count goes up by 1. If the current cell already shows the right letter (typed or revealed), nothing is revealed, no hint is counted and a short message says "This letter is already right: no hint used." (D-72 x). The player has **3 free hints** per crossword: the button reads "Reveal letter (N left)" and is hidden after the third hint (D-72 w).
- **Given** a new puzzle, **then** the timer shows 0:00 and starts on the **first letter typed or revealed**; it counts up (m:ss, h:mm:ss after an hour) and stops when the puzzle is solved.

*End and device lock*
- **Given** every cell holds the right letter, **then** the puzzle ends at once (no submit button): the timer stops, the grid becomes read-only and a result panel shows "Solved in 4:05", the hints used ("No hints" / "1 hint" / "3 hints"), Share and "Next crossword in HH:MM:SS" (to midnight UTC; at 0 it reads "Play the new crossword").
- **Given** all cells are filled but some are wrong, **then** a line says "Not quite: some letters are wrong." without showing which (Check word does that).
- **Given** Share, **then** it uses `js/share.*.js` (share sheet on phones, else copy plus "Copied") with the text of D-72 p and the site link.
- **Given** a reload or a return later the same UTC day, **then** the puzzle resumes from `localStorage` `brainrush.crossword` (`{date, cells, revealed, hints, startedAt, done, ...}`): letters, revealed cells, hints and the running time are kept. A finished puzzle shows the result screen, not the grid to play again. This lock is device-only (accepted, D-37 g). A save for an earlier date is discarded when today's puzzle loads; a corrupt save is discarded and the puzzle starts fresh.

*Language, layout, logging*
- **Given** the Arabic interface, **then** every crossword string (button, headings, Across/Down, tools, result, errors, share text, countdown) is in Arabic from `STRINGS.ar` (English from `STRINGS.en`), set with `textContent` only; the page is RTL but the **grid stays LTR** (`dir="ltr"`) so words read left to right and the arrows keep their direction. Switching the language mid-puzzle keeps the progress and the timer (`refreshScreen` branch).
- **Given** a 1366 × 768 screen (D-30), **then** a 13 × 13 grid, the tools, the timer and the active clue are visible without page scrolling (the clue lists may scroll inside their panel). **Given** a 375 px wide phone, **then** the grid fits the width without sideways scrolling, the active-clue bar is above it and the clue lists below. Text and controls meet WCAG AA contrast.
- **Given** the first letter typed or revealed on a device for a day's puzzle, **then** one line is posted to `api/game-log.php` with mode `crossword` (no name, `categories: all`); `api/game-log.php` accepts the mode, other unknown modes still → 400. No question stats are sent (as Daily).

*Docs*
- **Given** the docs, **then** `how-to-play.html` has a "Daily Crossword" section; `llms.txt` and `README.md` mention the mode (README: the migration); the privacy page's device-storage line includes the crossword progress (`brainrush.crossword`, kept on the device only); `docs/deploy-hostinger.md` says to run `sql/migrate-add-daily-crossword.sql` **before** uploading the code; `docs/spec.md` describes the mode and the API.
- *Dependencies / risks:* reuses the Daily day logic (`GAME_TIME_ZONE`, `seconds_to_next`), `daily_bank()` / `CELL_COLUMNS` and `choice_text()`. The local bank may give few candidate words; the dev logs the candidate count and simulates several dates. Constants `CROSSWORD_MIN_WORDS` (6), `CROSSWORD_MAX_WORDS` (10), `CROSSWORD_MAX_SIZE` (13) and `CROSSWORD_LAUNCH_DATE` sit at the top of `api/crossword.php`. New hashed assets need `git add` before the commit (hash hook). Follow-ups FU-87, FU-88.
- *PO acceptance (2026-11-20):* **ACCEPTED.** Test report `docs/sprints/us-58-test-report.md` (with "Re-test after fix loop 1"): 49 criteria, 46 PASS, 0 FAIL, 3 PENDING; BUG-58-01 to BUG-58-03 (Minor) fixed and re-tested. PENDING closed by the PO: C37 (WCAG) covered by the dev's 20 formula-measured pairs (lowest 5.04:1) and the ux-ui `verify_states` gate pass on `index.html` (142 element-states, AA); C48 (exact 13 × 13) covered by the driven 13 × 10 and 10 × 13 grids, which exercise each maximum dimension; C49 (phone share sheet) moves to the live smoke check (FU-90, `navigator.share` needs HTTPS). Developer open points decided in D-72 s to v. Owner steps: FU-89, FU-90.

## Epic E20 — Speed Round

### US-59 Speed Round (Should)
As a solo player, I want a fast mode where quick right answers earn me more time on one clock, so that every run is short, tense and makes me want to beat my best. *(Added 2026-11-25, owner request, D-73. Size M. Branch `speed-round`. No migration.)*

*Admin and entry*
- **Given** Admin > Game settings, **then** it has a checkbox "Speed Round" (setting `speed_enabled`, 0/1), loaded and saved like `crossword_enabled`. `PUT api/settings.php` with any other value → 400. A database with no `speed_enabled` row returns 0 (off), so no migration is needed.
- **Given** `speed_enabled` is 0, **then** the game page shows no Speed Round button. **Given** it is 1, **then** a "Speed Round" button (`#speed-open-btn`, high-voltage emoji before the label, D-73 z) shows in `.mode-extras` next to the Daily buttons, under the same rules (hidden in maintenance mode and in the Streak setup).
- The button has no NEW badge (owner change 2026-11-25, D-73 z).
- **Given** the button is tapped, **then** the first batch is loaded and the run starts with no name or setup. On this device's first run a card shows three lines ("Answer fast to earn time", "Wrong costs 3 s", "Every correct answer is a point") and Start; then a 3-2-1 countdown. Later runs show only the countdown. A failed load stays on setup with a message.

*Rules (D-73)*
- **Given** a new run, **then** the clock starts at 45 s (D-73 aa); it is one clock for the whole run and never goes above 60 s.
- **Given** a correct answer, **then** the score goes up by 1 and the clock gains +5 s (answered in 2 s or less), +3 s (4 s or less), +2 s (6 s or less) or +1 s (slower), up to the cap.
- **Given** a wrong answer, **then** the clock loses 3 s, the wrong pick shows red and the correct choice green; the score is unchanged and the run goes on.
- **Given** the clock reaches 0, **then** the run ends: on an open question it shows "Time's up!" and the correct choice, then the result.
- **Given** the numbers above, **then** they live in one constants block in `js/game.*.js` (`SPEED_START_S`, `SPEED_CAP_S`, `SPEED_TIERS`, `SPEED_WRONG_S`), marked "playtest and tune".

*Fair timing*
- **Given** a question, **then** its stopwatch and the clock start only when it is visible: for a picture question once the picture has loaded (or failed); the next queued pictures are fetched ahead.
- **Given** an answer, **then** the clock is frozen during the ~0.8 s feedback, and the next question's time starts when it is shown.
- **Given** the tab is hidden, **then** the run pauses under a "Paused / Tap to resume" card that covers the question; the paused time counts neither for the clock nor for the answer time.
- **Given** a reload mid-run, **then** the run comes back paused with the same time left (the score, the answer times and the open question kept).

*Questions, stats and logging*
- **Given** a run, **then** its questions come from `POST api/questions.php?streak=1` (all values, 3 choices, picture questions included, picked at random; the played-questions list is not sent, D-73 ac); a run never repeats a question (its questions so far are sent as `exclude`); `questions.php` is unchanged.
- **Given** an answered question, **then** it is **not** marked as seen (D-73 ac) and its outcome (correct or wrong) is sent to `api/question-stats.php`; the question on screen when the clock runs out sends nothing.
- **Given** a run starts, **then** one line is posted to `api/game-log.php` with mode `speed` (no name, `categories: all`).

*During play*
- **Given** an answer, **then** a floating "+N s" (green) or "−3 s" (red) shows by the clock, a speed label ("Lightning!", "Quick!", "Nice!", "Correct!") and the time change are announced in the polite live line, and the clock turns red under 10 s. Under reduced motion nothing moves (the texts still show).
- **Given** End run (or Android Back), **then** the clock is frozen while "End the run now?" is open; OK shows the result, Cancel goes on.

*Result*
- **Given** the run is over, **then** the result shows the big score, "New personal best!", "You matched your best (N)!", "N away from your best (B)" or "No correct answers this time" (no fastest or average line: owner change 2026-11-25, D-73 y).
- **Given** Play again (the primary button), **then** a new run starts with the countdown only. **Given** Share, **then** `BrainRushShare.share` sends "Mokh Rush Speed Round: 18 correct" (Arabic in Arabic) with the site link. **Given** New Game, **then** setup shows again.
- **Given** the personal best, **then** it is kept in `localStorage` `brainrush.speed` only; no leaderboard, no server table. *(The "no leaderboard, no server table" part is superseded by US-60 / D-76, 2026-11-25: the run's score goes to the shared Speed leaderboard. The personal best stays on the device.)*

*Saved runs*
- **Given** a saved Streak, Daily and Speed run, **then** a reload brings back only the one started last (`brainrush.lastSolo`). Starting a Speed Round, or leaving its result, never clears a saved `brainrush.streak` run.

*Language, layout, docs*
- **Given** the Arabic interface, **then** every Speed Round string comes from `speed.*` in `STRINGS.ar`, the page is RTL and signed seconds read left to right. **Given** a 375 px phone, **then** nothing scrolls sideways. Contrast meets WCAG AA.
- **Given** the docs, **then** `how-to-play.html` has a `#speed` section in its contents, `llms.txt`, README and the privacy page mention it, and `docs/deploy-hostinger.md` lists the upload (no migration; tick the setting in live Admin).
- *Dependencies / risks:* reuses `fetchStreakBatch`, `addToQueue`, the streak panel, `renderRunChoices` and the shared timer (new `extendDeadline`, a 10 s red threshold). The tiers make only answers of about 3 s or less gain time overall, so first runs may be short: the owner tunes after the local test. Follow-ups FU-91 to FU-99 (PO acceptance 2026-11-25, D-73 s to x).

### US-60 Speed Round leaderboard (Should)
As a Speed Round player, I want my score on a shared leaderboard under my name, so that I can see how I rank against everyone this week and of all time. *(Added 2026-11-25, owner request, D-76. Size M. Branch `speed-leaderboard`. Migration `sql/migrate-add-speed-scores.sql` before the code. Supersedes US-59's "no leaderboard, no server table" for the score; the personal best stays on the device.)*

*Server*
- **Given** `sql/migrate-add-speed-scores.sql` (idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`), **then** table `speed_scores` exists with the columns and keys of `streaks` (`id`, `name` VARCHAR(30), `streak` INT UNSIGNED = the run's correct answers, `created_at`, keys on `name` and `streak`); `sql/schema.sql` and `sql/schema-hosted.sql` include it.
- **Given** `api/speed-scores.php`, **then** it reuses `api/streaks.php` (`STREAKS_INCLUDE_ONLY`, `streak_board_request('speed_scores')`, `speed_scores` added to `STREAK_TABLES`): public `GET` (top 10 of all time; `?period=week` the current UTC week, D-46; any other period 400) and `POST {name, streak}` → 201 `{rank, best, week_rank, week_best}` (name 1–30 characters, streak 1–100000, else 400); admin-only `GET ?all=1` and `DELETE ?id=` (401/403 without the login). Best run per name (case-insensitive), the earliest run wins ties. Without the table 503 with a clear message.

*Name step*
- **Given** the Speed Round button (or Play again), **then** after the first batch loads the Speed popup shows a "Challenger name" field (required, at most 30 characters, the Streak's checks and messages, inline error, `dir="auto"` in Arabic) with Back and Start, **before** the 3-2-1 countdown; on this device's first run the three rules lines show above it. Enter submits. Every run asks.
- **Given** the field, **then** it is prefilled with the run's own name (a reload on the name step), else the name last used in a Streak or Speed run (`localStorage` `brainrush.challengerName`), else the first Players-row name.
- **Given** Back on the name step (button or Android Back), **then** no run starts (as Back on the rules card before).
- **Given** Start, **then** the cleaned name is saved with the run (`brainrush.speed` version 2) and survives a reload like the rest of the run. **Given** a version 1 save, **then** its personal best is kept, a run in it (no name) is dropped and its result is shown without the leaderboard.

*Result and leaderboard*
- **Given** a run with 1 or more correct answers ends, **then** it is posted to `api/speed-scores.php` and the result shows "Saving your score..." then the rank line ("You are #2 this week and #7 of all time.", with "Your best is still N." when an earlier run of that name was better). Play again and New Game wait while it saves. A run with 0 correct answers is not saved: "Only runs with 1 or more correct answers go on the leaderboard."
- **Given** the save fails, **then** the result shows "Your score could not be saved: <reason>" with Try again; a reload shows the result and sends it again; leaving with Play again or New Game asks "Your score of N was not saved. Leave it unsaved?" first.
- **Given** "🏆 Leaderboard" on the result, **then** the leaderboard screen shows the **Speed leaderboard**: the Streak board's "This week" / "All time" tabs and top 10 rows ("Name · 18 correct · 25 Nov 2026"), the player's row highlighted, the headline "Name: 18 correct!" and the rank line, and Back (to the result). There is no leaderboard button on the main setup screen (owner removed the 🏆 beside Speed Round, 2026-11-26). No ad on it (the ad stays on the Streak board, D-41).
- **Given** Admin, **then** a **Speed leaderboard** panel lists every saved run (name, correct answers, date and time, newest first) with Delete (confirm first).
- Unchanged from US-59: personal best, Share (score only), beeps, the shaking clock, no played-list marks, `speed_enabled` controls the whole mode. The game log line stays `speed: - | categories: all` (no name).

*Language, layout, docs*
- **Given** the Arabic interface, **then** every new string comes from `speed.*` in `STRINGS.ar`. **Given** 1366x768, 375 px and 320 px, **then** nothing scrolls sideways and the result fits 1366x768.
- **Given** the docs, **then** `how-to-play.html` (#speed), `llms.txt`, `privacy.html`, README, `docs/spec.md` and `docs/deploy-hostinger.md` (migration first, then the upload) describe the leaderboard and the name.

## Epic E21 — Tournament

### US-61 Tournament mode (Should)
As a host, I want a knockout tournament for 3 to 8 contestants, where each match is a short board between two of them and the winner goes through, so that a bigger group can play to one champion and keep the final standings. *(Added 2026-11-26, owner request, D-77. Size M–L. Branch `tournament-mode`. No migration; one server line in `api/game-log.php`.)*

*Setup*
- **Given** the setup screen, **then** the mode toggle has a third choice **Tournament** next to Players and Teams; its add button reads "Add contestant" and at most **8** inputs can exist ("Add" disabled at 8). Name rules are unchanged (required, unique, 1–30 characters; D-02, D-22).
- **Given** Tournament mode, **then** the palette has **8** colours (the 6 of US-03 plus 2 new ones), all distinct, each entry auto-assigned a free one, the swatch cycling through free colours. The 2 new colours are offered in Tournament mode only (Players and Teams keep the 6 of US-03); they are not grey or gold and are clearly different from the six, and the `#0b1026` ink meets WCAG AA 4.5:1 on them (FU-75).
- **Given** fewer than 3 names in Tournament mode, **when** the host clicks Start, **then** it does not start and an inline error says "A tournament needs at least 3 contestants" (a field error, D-23). **Given** 7 or 8 entries, **when** the host switches to Players or Teams, **then** Start is blocked as D-07 says.
- **Given** Tournament mode, **then** the timer is chosen as for a normal game and used for **every** match; the setup categories are **switched off** (greyed, the ticks kept for Players and Teams) with the note "In a tournament, the categories are chosen before each match.", and Start needs no category (D-77 ab).
- **Given** the bracket, **when** the host presses **Start match**, **then** a popup "Choose the categories" opens for that match ("Semi-final 1: Sara v Omar. Choose up to 3 categories for this match."), listing the playable categories read now, with Surprise me and Kilo Meshakkal; **1 to 3** can be ticked (the unticked boxes lock at 3; the popup's Start match is disabled with none ticked); the columns follow the tick order; a "Surprise me!" pick is drawn for that match only ("?" until one of its questions is opened, US-31). The popup starts with the last match's ticks (this visit only). Cancel, Escape and Android Back close it and start nothing (D-77 ab).
- **Given** Tournament mode, **then** the Daily, Crossword and Speed buttons stay as they are (D-47 e) and the Streak button is hidden, as in Teams mode.

*Draw and bracket*
- **Given** valid setup, **when** the host clicks Start, **then** a **random draw** fills a bracket of **4** (3 or 4 entries: Semi-finals, Final) or **8** (5 to 8 entries: Quarter-finals, Semi-finals, Final), and the **bracket screen** shows it. Empty spots are **byes**.
- **Given** the byes, **then** they sit in the second slot of first-round matches in this fixed order: bracket of 4, match 1; bracket of 8, matches 1, 4, 2 (alternating halves). So with 3 entries: A v bye, B v C; with 5: A v bye, B v bye, C v D, E v bye. Two byes never meet each other in round 1 (with 5 entries two bye entrants can meet in a semi-final, D-77 u). The shuffle decides who sits where, not where the byes are.
- **Given** a bye match, **then** it shows "Name – bye" and the entry is already in the next round; no board is played.
- **Given** the bracket screen before the first match has started, **then** a **Shuffle** button redraws the whole bracket; once the first match starts, Shuffle is gone.
- **Given** the bracket screen, **then** it shows each round as a column with match cards (colour swatch, name, score once played, winner marked by more than colour), highlights the **next match** (first round top to bottom, then the next round), and has **Start match** and **End tournament**.
- **Given** End tournament, **then** a confirm says "End the tournament? It will not be saved."; OK goes to setup and the tournament is dropped; Cancel stays.

*Match*
- **Given** Start match, **then** a new board for the two contestants is drawn with `POST questions.php?board=1` (the categories chosen in the popup and the played list as `seen`, so unseen questions come first); the browser keeps only the **point values ticked in Admin > Point values > Tournament board** that are enabled (D-77 aa); with none ticked, or none of the ticked ones enabled, the **lowest 3 enabled point values** (`TOURNAMENT_ROWS` = 3; all enabled values when fewer than 3 are enabled). The cut is made before the Kilo Meshakkal column and the double cell are dealt; the setting and the enabled values are read at each match start (D-12 per match).
- **Given** Admin > Point values, **then** under the point value list a **Tournament board: point values** group shows one checkbox per point value (disabled ones marked "(disabled)"), saved with its own **Save tournament board** button (not with Save settings) as `tournament_points` (an ascending comma list, "" = none ticked; at most 20 whole values 1 to 1000000, else 400). No migration: a row in `settings`; a missing row means none ticked.
- **Given** a match, **then** it plays on the existing board and question screens with the setup timer: scores start at 0, each contestant has 3 fresh helpers (D-36), the board has its own double cell (D-40), and Correct/Wrong, Report, read-aloud, stats (US-47) and the played list work as on a normal board. A Swap may bring a question at a value that is not on the short board; it is scored at that value (D-36 d).
- **Given** End Game (D-09 confirm while cells remain) or a full board, **then** the match ends: the higher score wins (negative scores included).
- **Given** the two scores are level, **then** **Golden Goal** runs for the two contestants **whatever** `final_round_enabled` says, with no "Skip to results": the bet cap is their score or the largest value on this match board, whichever is higher (D-48, D-49 b); rounds repeat with a new question until one leads (D-51 e). **Given** no question is left for another round, **then** the winner is drawn at random and shown as "Coin toss: <name> goes through".
- **Given** the match is decided, **then** a match result view shows "<Name> wins <score>–<score>" (and "after Golden Goal" or "on a coin toss" when so) with **Continue**, which returns to the bracket with the winner moved on and the loser marked out. It has no Share and no New Game.

*Final results and standings*
- **Given** the final is decided, **then** the **tournament results screen** shows "<Name> wins the tournament!", the standings and every played match with its score.
- **Given** standings, **then** places are 1st (champion), 2nd (final loser), **joint 3rd** (semi-final losers), **joint 5th** (quarter-final losers), plain shared (no order by score), listed in bracket order. Bye advances are not losses. With 3 entries the standings are 1st, 2nd, 3rd (one semi-final loser); with 5 they are 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 3rd, 5th.
- **Given** Share, **then** `BrainRushShare.share` sends e.g. "Sara won the Mokh Rush tournament! 2nd Omar, 3rd Ali and Mona" plus the site link (US-46 rules: share sheet on touch, else Copied).
- **Given** the tournament results, **then** they stay (also over a reload) until **New game**, which shows setup (D-15) and drops the tournament; finished tournaments are not kept after that (no Past tournaments list, D-77 z).

*Refresh, Back and log*
- **Given** a reload on the bracket, a match board or question, a match Golden Goal, a match result or the tournament results, **then** the tournament comes back where it was (D-04 and D-25 for an open question). A save change follows D-28 (version bump, or optional fields as D-56 a); a broken save discards to setup.
- **Given** Android Back (D-43), **then** on the bracket screen it asks as End tournament; on a match board or question it works as in a normal game; on a match Golden Goal it never ends the round with a tie (ignored on the offer and bet steps, asks before closing an open question as D-43 a); on the tournament results screen it goes to setup like New game.
- **Given** Start, **then** **one** game log line is posted with mode `tournament` and all entry names, no categories (`tournament: <names> | categories: chosen per match`, D-77 ab); matches are not logged. `api/game-log.php` accepts mode `tournament` with 3 to 8 names; any other count gives 400.

*Language, layout, docs*
- **Given** the Arabic interface, **then** every new string comes from `tournament.*` in both tables of `js/i18n.*.js` (D-60), AI-drafted (D-58 a, FU-107); the bracket mirrors in RTL.
- **Given** 8 entries at 1366x768, **then** the bracket screen and the tournament results fit with no page scroll (D-30); a match board fits as a normal board. **Given** 375 px and 320 px, **then** the bracket stacks rounds vertically with no sideways scroll. Contrast meets WCAG AA.
- **Given** the docs, **then** `how-to-play.html` has a `#tournament` section in its contents, `llms.txt`, `about.html`, README and `docs/spec.md` mention it, `privacy.html` says contestant names of finished tournaments are kept on the device, and `docs/deploy-hostinger.md` lists the upload (no migration).
- *Dependencies / risks:* reuses `beginGame`, the board and question screens, Golden Goal (forced and without Skip in a match) and `BrainRushShare`. Short boards mean small banks repeat sooner across matches; only the played list (unseen first) avoids repeats, there is no `exclude` on the board API. New hashed assets need `git add` before the commit (hash hook). Follow-ups FU-107 to FU-109.

### US-62 Mokh Bot in Tournament mode (Should)
As a host, I want a weak computer contestant, "Mokh Bot 🤖", to take one of the byes of an uneven tournament, so that the contestant who would get a bye plays a real, easy match instead. *(Added 2026-11-26, owner change request, D-78. Size M. Branch `tournament-bot`. No migration, no server change.)*

*Draw*
- **Given** 3, 5, 6 or 7 human contestants, **when** the host clicks Start or Shuffle, **then** the bracket size and the bye slots come from the human count as in US-61 (D-77 g), and **one** bot entry takes **one** of those bye slots at random (second slot, a real match); any other byes stay byes. 3 humans: the bot fills the only bye; 5: one of 3; 6: one of 2; 7: the only one. **Given** 4 or 8 humans, **then** there is no bot.
- **Given** the bot entry, **then** its id is the human count + 1, it takes a free palette colour, and its name is "Mokh Bot 🤖" ("مخ بوت 🤖" in Arabic, `tournament.botName`), with " 2" after it when a human already has that name; the name follows a language switch.
- **Given** the game log line at Start, **then** it holds the human names only (no server change).
- **Given** the bot wins a match, **then** it goes through; it can win the tournament and appears in the standings, the played matches and the share text like any entry.

*A match with the bot (turn-based; human v human matches unchanged)*
- **Given** a match with the bot starts, **then** a coin flip overlay (a spinning coin in the two colours) lands on "Sara starts" or "Mokh Bot 🤖 starts" and fades out; the starter is random. Under `prefers-reduced-motion` the coin shows the result face without spinning. Once it has closed, a reload does not play it again (`coinShown`).
- **Given** a match with the bot, **then** a banner on the board says whose turn it is ("Sara's turn") in that contestant's colour, and the turn passes after every closed question (Back to Board, Skip). There is no steal.
- **Given** the human's turn, **then** the host opens any cell; on the question the bot's row is greyed with "Waiting" and no buttons; only the human's Correct, Wrong and helpers work.
- **Given** the bot's turn, **then** the board is locked for the host; after about 1.2 s the bot picks a random open cell (a short highlight) and it opens as a click would (the double score popup included). The human's row is locked ("Mokh Bot 🤖's question"). After 3 to 6 s (always before the timer ends; after the double score popup is closed) the bot's row turns from "Thinking…" to "Mokh Bot 🤖 says: (answer)", and about 1.5 s later the bot is scored by itself (Correct +points, doubled on the double cell; Wrong −points). Back to Board is disabled until then (developer choice, D-78 l).
- **Given** the bot's answer, **then** it is right with a chance of about 35% on the lowest row down to about 20% on the highest; right: the real answer, cut at its first bracket, in the question's shown language; wrong: one of the question's stored wrong options (the 3 choices rule), else "I don't know…". The roll is saved on the open question (`botRoll`), so a reload or a language switch shows the same answer (an answer already shown comes back at once). The bot never uses helpers.
- **Given** a level match with the bot, **then** in Golden Goal the bot locks its own bet when its turn comes (0 to half its limit, rounded to the nearest hundred, never above the limit) and answers the question in the same bubble, then marks itself.
- **Given** a reload on any screen of a bot match, **then** it resumes from the saved turn, roll and marks; any pending bot action is stopped on every screen change.

*Language, layout, docs*
- **Given** the Arabic interface, **then** the new strings come from both tables of `js/i18n.*.js`, AI-drafted (D-58 a, FU-110), and the banner, rows and bubble mirror in RTL. **Given** 1366x768, 375 px and 320 px, **then** the board, question and coin flip fit as a normal match (D-30).
- **Given** the docs, **then** `how-to-play.html` (#tournament), `docs/spec.md` and `llms.txt` describe the bot.
- *Dependencies / risks:* builds on US-61. `bot` is an optional entry field and `turn`, `coinShown` and `botRoll` are optional game fields (D-56 a), so `TOURNAMENT_VERSION` stays 1 and older saves load. The bot's own questions send no question stats (D-78 p). Follow-ups FU-110 to FU-112.
- *Changed by US-63 (D-79):* the bot's accuracy now comes from the Admin slider (no row slope, the same dice in Golden Goal), its Golden Goal bet is accuracy x limit, and it picks its cell by its accuracy and the score instead of at random.

### US-63 Mokh Bot accuracy meter and smart board play (Should)
As the admin, I want to set how good Mokh Bot is with one accuracy slider, and I want the bot to choose its cells sensibly, so that a match against it is as easy or as hard as I like. *(Added 2026-11-26, owner change request, D-79. Size S. Branch `bot-accuracy`. No migration; one new setting in `api/settings.php`.)*

*Admin*
- **Given** Admin > Point values, **then** under "Tournament board" there is a "Mokh Bot accuracy" slider from 0% to 100% in steps of 10, with the value shown live ("70%") and a hint "Out of 10 answers: 7 right, 2 wrong, 1 I don't know." It has its own **Save bot accuracy** button, which saves only `bot_accuracy`.
- **Given** the setting was never saved, **then** the slider shows 30% and the game uses 30%.
- **Given** `PUT settings.php` with `bot_accuracy` outside the whole numbers 0 to 10 (for example 11, -1 or 5.5), **then** the answer is 400 and nothing is saved. The API stores right tickets out of 10 (7 = 70%).

*The dice*
- **Given** a bot match starts, **then** the game reads `bot_accuracy` once with the match's settings and keeps it on the match board (`botTickets`) for the whole match, also after a reload.
- **Given** the bot answers a board or Golden Goal question, **then** it rolls a 10-face dice. At A% the faces are, in this order: A/10 right, then the larger half of the rest wrong, then the smaller half "I don't know". 30%: 3 right, 4 wrong, 3 "I don't know"; 70%: 7 right, 2 wrong, 1 "I don't know". The same dice is used on every row and in Golden Goal.
- **Given** a right face, **then** the bot says the real answer and is scored Correct. **Given** a wrong face, **then** it says a stored wrong option (the wrong faces alternate `wrong_1`, `wrong_2`) and is scored Wrong; with no stored options it says "I don't know…". **Given** an "I don't know" face, **then** it says "I don't know…" and is scored Wrong (it loses the points).
- **Given** 0%, **then** the bot is never right; **given** 100%, **then** it is always right.

*Golden Goal bet*
- **Given** the bot's bet, **then** it is the accuracy times its limit, rounded to the nearest 100 and never above the limit: limit 1000 at 70% gives 700; limit 200 at 30% gives 100 (only when the admin enables just the 200 row; on the default board, rows 200 to 600, the smallest limit is 600, which gives 200). It is no longer random.

*Smart board play*
- **Given** the bot's turn, **then** it picks the point value first: above 50% the **highest** open value; at 50% or less the **highest** when it is behind its opponent and the **lowest** when it is ahead or level (checked before every pick). Then a random category among the open cells of that value.
- **Given** the hidden double cell and the hidden values of the Kilo Meshakkal boxes, **then** the bot does not use them: it picks among the cells whose value shows, and opens a Kilo Meshakkal box (at random) only when no other cell is open (developer choice, D-79 j).

*Compatibility and docs*
- **Given** a tournament saved before US-63 (no `botTickets`), **then** it loads and the bot plays at 30%. `TOURNAMENT_VERSION` stays 1 (D-56 a). Human v human matches are unchanged.
- **Given** the docs, **then** `how-to-play.html` (#tournament-bot), `docs/spec.md`, `llms.txt` and `docs/deploy-hostinger.md` describe the meter and the smart play.
- *Dependencies / risks:* builds on US-62. The Admin page and the guide are English only, so there are no new Arabic strings and no review follow-up. `api/settings.php` must be uploaded with the new js (an old server answers 400 to the new Save).
