| D-64 | Rename to Mokh Rush (CR `rename-mokh-rush`) | Owner decision 2026-11-11 (plan of 2026-11-02, refreshed 2026-11-11). (a) **Why:** other games already use the name "BrainRush"; the owner wanted an Arab Gen-Z name. A web search (2026-11-02) found no game called Mokh Rush (nearest neighbour: Mkhmkh مخمخ, a Kuwaiti party trivia app). (b) **Name:** English "Mokh Rush" (always "Mokh", no "Mo5" variant); the Arabic interface says "مخ رش". One key `app.name` in `js/i18n.*.js` holds both. The Arabic share and Daily lines (`share.won`, `share.tie`, `daily.score`) use "مخ رش". On the game page the header h1, the footer brand and the tab title follow the language (Arabic: "مخ رش" alone as the tab title); this amends D-59 d for the game page's `<title>` only. Meta, Open Graph and JSON-LD stay English. The static pages (guide, About, Contact, Privacy, Terms, Admin, Integration Quiz, `offline.html`) have no Arabic version and say "Mokh Rush". (c) **Logo:** Concept A **revision A2** (a forward-leaning gold brain profile with three cut-in folds, cerebellum and stem, and three trailing streaks; `docs/design/logo-concepts/concept-a2*.svg`, `revision-a2.html`), approved by the owner after the design critic rejected the first Concept A drawing. `img/logo.svg` is the app icon tile (replaced in place). The page header uses the **frameless mark** `img/mokh-mark.svg` (about 1.5:1, height-driven at 2.2rem), and the browser tab uses a **dedicated small-size favicon** `img/mokh-favicon.svg` (32-unit drawing) plus its PNG. The mark points right (forward motion), against the Arabic reading direction; it is deliberately **not mirrored** in the Arabic interface. New SVG files: `img/mokh-mark.svg`, `img/mokh-favicon.svg`; the PNGs get **new file names** because of the 7-day image cache: `img/mokh-favicon-32.png`, `mokh-apple-touch-icon.png`, `mokh-icon-192.png`, `mokh-icon-512.png`, `mokh-icon-maskable-512.png`, `mokh-logo-512.png`, `mokh-logo-wordmark.png` (frameless mark + Latin "Mokh Rush", no pill), `mokh-og-image.png` (no edge frame, frameless mark, tagline "Team trivia, buzzer speed"). The old PNGs stay on the server for old shared links but nothing references them. (d) **Scope:** only what players see: page titles, h1, footers, body copy, og/twitter/JSON-LD names, share texts, `manifest.webmanifest`, the Android app name and launcher name, the connector's titles and texts (`api/mcp.php`, `mcp/trivia-card.html`), the admin login realm, `llms.txt`, comment headers, README and the spec title. Past sprint reports, question-set reviews and SQL headers stay as written (history); server log prefixes (`BrainRush API:`) stay so old and new logs grep the same. (e) **Kept on purpose:** localStorage keys `brainrush.*`, JS globals `BrainRushShare`, `BrainRushSpeech`, `BrainRushI18n`, `brainrushApp`, service-worker cache names `brainrush-offline-*`, the DB name `brainrush`, the GitHub repo, agent file names and the scheduled task name, the MCP tool names `show_brainrush_trivia` / `brainrush_trivia_questions`, URI `ui://brainrush/trivia-card` and server name `brainrush-trivia`, and `api/config.example.php`. (f) **For now** the domain `brainrush.integration.ninja` (all URLs and canonical links) and the Android package `ninja.integration.brainrush` with keystore `brainrush-upload` stay; changing them is a separate decision. FU-68 to FU-74. **PO accepted 2026-11-11** (test and re-test all PASS). Server `error_log` prefixes staying "BrainRush" is agreed (not player-visible). BUG-CR-01 (one-off 500 on the first local load, not reproduced, diff unrelated) is closed; if a 500 recurs, the owner checks the PHP error log. Deferred: the mod's titles (FU-73) and the quoted gate output and critic verdict on A2 (FU-74). |
| D-65 | Free second chance in the Streak Challenge (US-54) | Owner decisions 2026-11-11. (a) Every Streak Challenge run gets **one** second chance, free; (b) it covers a wrong pick and a time-out; (c) it keeps the streak and replaces the missed question with a new one; (d) no leaderboard marker or separate board: the owner expects all players to use it over time; (e) Streak Challenge only, not the Integration Quiz or Daily Challenge; (f) how to earn it is designed later; (g) US-51 (rewarded ad) stays as a possible extra chance. *(2026-11-16: (f) proposed as US-57, D-69. 2026-11-17: (a) and (f) replaced by D-69 as amended: chances are earned, none is free.)* |
| D-68 | Challenge a friend (US-56) | PO decisions 2026-11-16, owner request; **US-56 cancelled 2026-11-18 (owner), FU-80 and FU-81 cancelled; kept as a record only.** (a) **Link:** only a random server-stored id (`?challenge=<id>`), so the URL reveals no questions or answers; the row holds the challenger name (already public on the leaderboard), streak, timer, ordered question ids and creation time, nothing about the friend. (b) **Offered** after a run with a streak of 1 or more, and again after a challenge run (a new challenge from the friend's run). (c) **Same run:** same questions in the same order, the challenger's timer, no helpers, the second-chance rule in force when the friend plays; a used chance moves to the next listed question. Choices are rebuilt, so their order can differ (as D-47 g). (d) **After the list** the run continues with normal random Streak questions. (e) **Deleted questions or disabled values** are skipped with a note; none left = expired. (f) **Expiry** 30 days (proposal, FU-81), deleted on the next POST. (g) **A game or run in progress** is never discarded by a link; a notice asks to reopen the link afterwards. (h) The challenger's run counts on the leaderboard as today; the friend's run counts as a normal run (proposal, FU-80). (i) **No Admin panel** for challenges: expiry handles cleanup. (j) Result lines: "You beat Sara's 14!", "Level with Sara: 14 each.", "Sara still leads: 14 vs 9." (k) Rate limit on creating challenges: proposal in FU-81. US-56. |
| D-69 | Earn the second chance (US-57) | PO proposal 2026-11-16, owner request; **amended 2026-11-17 with the owner's rule (FU-82 closed)**; replaces D-65 a and resolves D-65 f. (a) A run **starts with 0** chances; (b) **5** correct answers earn the **1st** chance, then the counter restarts and **10 more** earn the **2nd**; at most **2** are earned per run and the counter then stops; only correct answers count; (c) **using a chance restarts the counter at 0** and never resets the streak (as US-54); (d) the screen shows "Next chance: 3/5" (hidden once both are earned) and, once one is earned, the chances held ("1 chance", "2 chances", "No chances left"); the earning answer reads "+1 second chance!"; the reused "Second chance!" popup says how many are held; (e) refresh keeps the chances and counter; US-54 saves map to 1 earned (unused) or 1 earned and used, counter 0; an invalid value discards the run; (f) Integration Quiz and Daily Challenge unchanged; no leaderboard marker (D-65 d); (g) client-only, numbers as named constants; (h) a challenge (US-56) uses the same rule for both players: the rule in force when each one plays; (i) US-51 stays separate; how a rewarded chance fits the cap is decided when it is unblocked. *(The first proposal, start with 1 and earn 1 every 10 up to 2, was not taken. 2026-11-17: the held count text of (d) replaced by hearts, D-71.)* US-57. |
| D-70 | US-57 acceptance details | PO decisions 2026-11-17 at the acceptance of US-57. (a) **Held count only after the 1st earn:** a fresh run shows only "Next chance: 0/5"; "1 chance", "2 chances" or "No chances left" appear from the first earned chance on ("No chances left" on a run that never had one reads as a loss). Once both are earned only the held count shows. (b) **Arabic earn line** says "حصلت على فرصة ثانية!" without a signed "+1" (same meaning, no bidi isolate needed); English keeps "+1 second chance!". Part of the native review (FU-84). (c) **Counter on its own line under "Streak N"**, aligned with the count, instead of on the same line: the owner said "beside the streak"; one line does not fit 375 px with the held count, and the AC says "under". Accepted; the owner confirms in the local test (FU-83) and can ask for a same-line layout on wide screens as a small change. (d) **US-54 saves** upgrade on load to 1 earned (unused) or 1 earned and used, counter 0, `earnedAt` 0; a pre-US-54 save loads at 0/5. At most one in-flight run per device gets the old free chance; accepted (D-56 a pattern). (e) **Earn line from state:** "+1 second chance!" is derived from `chances.earnedAt` (the streak at the last earn), not a stored flag (US57-01); saves where `earnedAt` or `progress` do not fit the streak are discarded (US-33). (f) **Not covered, accepted:** the Integration Quiz path to 5 correct answers could not run locally (2 questions in the local bank); the code guard and the tested miss-ends-the-run case are enough to accept, the live check is part of FU-85. Native Arabic review: FU-84 (D-58 a). Screen reader reading of the `aria-live="polite"` counter: checked in markup only, FU-86. (g) **O-3** (a saved run vanished once in the tester's first pass) not reproduced in a 4-seed random-play test; not a blocker; the owner watches for it in FU-83. (h) About 27 local `question_stats` rows from the tester's first pass stay (local only; "Reset all stats", D-57, clears them if wanted, as D-63 b). US-57. *(2026-11-17: (a) replaced by D-71, hearts.)* |
| D-71 | US-57 CR: second-chance hearts | Owner request 2026-11-17; replaces the held count text of D-69 d and D-70 a. (a) The Streak Challenge always shows **CHANCE_MAX (2) hearts** from the start of the run, not in the Integration Quiz or Daily Challenge; red = a chance held (`chancesLeft`), gray = not earned yet or used (a used heart looks like an unearned one); red fills from the inline start (the right in Arabic). (b) "Next chance: x/5" / "x/10" stays next to the hearts while a goal is open; "1 chance", "2 chances" and "No chances left" are gone (keys `chance.held`, `chance.none` removed; the popup keeps its own plural text). (c) Inline SVG hearts (no emoji, no image file), colours from new tokens `--heart-on` (#ff4d5e: 5.1:1 on the panel, 4.4:1 on the Christmas panel) and `--heart-off` (#6c7599: 3.7:1 and 3.2:1); a red heart also has a small white shine, so held and empty differ by more than colour. (d) Animations only on the live event (earn in `streakAnswer`, use in `nextStreakQuestion(true)`): a pop (scale and glow, 0.5 s) and a break (shake, crack, shrink, fade to gray, 0.55 s); transforms only (no layout shift); none on refresh, re-render or language switch; with `prefers-reduced-motion` only the colour changes. (e) Accessibility: the hearts are one `role="img"` with `aria-label` "Second chances: 1 of 2" (Arabic AI-drafted, D-58 a); the SVGs are `aria-hidden`; the `aria-live` line is rebuilt only when its content changes, so a change is announced once. (f) **PO acceptance 2026-11-17** (58 PASS / 0 FAIL): the counter text first and the hearts at the end edge under the streak number, the hearts staying alone once both are earned: accepted, the owner confirms the look in FU-83. The gray at 3.2:1 on the Christmas panel meets the 3:1 non-text minimum: accepted. H-01, the line staying with gray hearts after a run ends at 0 chances: intended (the hearts show the run's final state). H-02, the reduced-motion fix made after the test (`node --check` only): accepted, the owner checks it in FU-83. H-03, a language switch may read the line once in the new language: accepted. I-01, the 3 local `daily_challenge` rows from opening the Daily: normal app behaviour, left in place. The real Christmas Admin toggle (not switched by the tester to avoid a DB write) goes to FU-83. US-57 ACs rewritten for the hearts; FU-83, FU-84, FU-85 and FU-86 updated. US-57. |
| D-72 | Daily Crossword (US-58) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-20:** (a) **Daily**: one shared puzzle per UTC day for everyone, like the Daily Challenge (US-43, D-46). (b) **Clues from the existing question bank**: clue = question text, grid word = the cleaned answer; no new content table. (c) **English letters only** in the grid (A–Z); clues readable in English or Arabic with the per-clue toggle. (d) **Auto-generated**: the server builds the grid on the first request of the day and stores it (`daily_crossword`). **PO choices 2026-11-20 (dev details the owner did not set; can be changed by a small CR):** (e) **Cleaning and limits** as in US-58: cut at the first " (", drop a leading "The ", strip spaces / hyphens / apostrophes, fold accents, uppercase, keep 3–10 letters; text questions at enabled point values; one question per word; a word found in its own clue is skipped. 6–10 words, at most 13 × 13. (f) **Stored layout** holds question ids, positions and the word letters; the clue text is read at request time so Admin typo fixes show. A question deleted after the day's grid was built keeps its word in the grid and its clue reads "Clue removed: use Reveal letter." (accepted edge case, one day at most). (g) **Clue language**: each clue starts in the interface language (Arabic when the interface is Arabic and the question has Arabic text, else English); the toggle is per clue and is not saved (a reload shows the default again). Clue text is shown with its length "(5)"; the category name shows small in the active-clue bar only; points are not shown. (h) **No score in points**: the result is the solve time and the hints used; no leaderboard (optional later, FU-88). (i) **Reveal letter** costs 1 hint per letter, no time penalty, no cap; revealed letters are locked and marked. (j) **Check word** is free, unlimited, only marks wrong letters of the active word, and is not shown in the result. (k) **Timer** counts up from the first letter typed or revealed, wall-clock from `startedAt` (time away from the page counts, as the Daily's wall-clock deadline); no pause. (l) **Auto-finish** when every cell is right; a full but wrong grid says "Not quite: some letters are wrong." without detail; correct words are not marked until the end (Check word is the way to test a word). (m) **Setting scope**: `crossword_enabled` only shows or hides the button (off by default, missing row = off); the API stays public like `daily.php`, so a device with a saved puzzle is not broken by switching it off. (n) **#N** = days since `CROSSWORD_LAUNCH_DATE` + 1 (at least 1, as D-62 b); the constant is set to the planned live date, and the owner moves it in the PR if the deploy is later, so live starts at #1. (o) **Game log**: one `crossword` line per device per day, sent on the first letter typed or revealed (not on just opening), no name; no question stats (as Daily). No Report button and no read-aloud on the crossword in this story. (p) **Share text**: English "Mokh Rush Crossword #12: solved in 4:05 with 2 hints" ("with 1 hint", "with no hints"), plus the site link; Arabic AI-drafted "كلمات مخ رش المتقاطعة #12: حُلّت في 4:05 مع 2 تلميح" (wording and plural forms to the native review, FU-87). (q) **Navigation**: "Back" returns to the setup screen and keeps the progress; Android Back on the crossword screen does the same, with no confirm (nothing is lost). A save for an earlier date is dropped when today's puzzle loads (yesterday's unfinished puzzle cannot be finished). (r) **Clicking a cell** with words both ways keeps the current direction; a second click on the same cell switches it. **PO decisions at acceptance 2026-11-20:** (s) **Active-clue bar at every width** (not only ≤ 600 px): accepted; at 1366 × 768 it keeps the active clue and its EN/AR toggle in view while the lists scroll; the US-58 AC is amended. (t) **Arabic share line with proper plurals** (مع تلميح واحد / مع تلميحين / مع 3 تلميحات …; no hints has its own line) replaces the draft "مع 2 تلميح" of (p); final wording in FU-87. (u) **Self-clue rule stays a plain substring** (e.g. ART is skipped when the clue contains "party"): accepted as safe; it costs a few candidates (533 left locally, 10 words on every tested date). Change to a whole-word match only if days start failing the 6-word minimum. (v) **`CROSSWORD_LAUNCH_DATE`** stays '2026-11-20' in the branch; the owner sets it to the real live date in the PR before the merge (FU-90), so live starts at #1. Extra strings beyond the AC (the hints count in the head, the Check word feedback lines) are accepted and go to FU-87. **Owner change 2026-11-20 during the local test:** (w) **3 free hints** per crossword, replacing "no cap" in (i): the Reveal letter button shows the hints left ("Reveal letter (2 left)") and is hidden once all 3 are used; the result and share line still show the hints used. (x) **Reveal on a letter that is already right costs nothing** (owner, same day): nothing is revealed, no hint is counted, and the message "This letter is already right: no hint used." shows; this replaces the "reveal the first empty or wrong cell of the word instead" part of the AC. Edge case accepted: a deleted question's clue (f) still says "use Reveal letter" after the 3 hints are gone. (y) **Excluded categories** (owner, 2026-11-21): Egyptians, Who Am I?, Flags and Landmarks never give crossword words (`CROSSWORD_EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES` in `api/crossword.php`, matched by name since ids differ between databases); locally 533 → 452 candidates, 10 words on each of 60 tested dates. A day's grid already stored keeps its words. US-58. |
| D-73 | Speed Round (US-59) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-25 (approved plan):** (a) **Score = number of correct answers**; time is the fuel that keeps the run going. (b) **One clock** for the whole run: 30 s at the start (changed to **45 s** by the owner, D-73 aa), never above 60 s. (c) **Time by speed** for a correct answer: 2 s or less +5 s, 4 s or less +3 s, 6 s or less +2 s, slower +1 s. (d) **Wrong answer** −3 s and the correct choice is shown; no score change, the run goes on. (e) **End at 0**; a question open when the clock runs out counts as time's up and sends no difficulty stat (it is the run ending, not the question being hard); correct and wrong answers are sent (US-47) and marked seen (marking seen dropped by the owner, D-73 ac). (f) **Personal best only**, on the device (`brainrush.speed`); no leaderboard, no table, no migration (*the "no leaderboard, no table" part is superseded by D-76 / US-60 (2026-11-25): the score goes to the shared Speed leaderboard; the personal best stays on the device*). (g) **Anti-cheat as Streak** (browser-checked, D-37 g); with no shared board, cheating only affects the cheater's own record (*superseded by D-76 d: with the shared board a faked score can reach it, the accepted D-37 limitation; the admin deletes it in the Speed leaderboard panel*). (h) **Questions** = the Streak pool (`questions.php?streak=1`: all values, 3 choices, picture questions included). (i) **Fair timing:** the clock and the answer stopwatch run only while the question is visible (a picture after it loads; next pictures prefetched), freeze during the ~0.8 s feedback, pause on a hidden tab ("Paused, tap to resume", the paused time not counted) and keep the time left over a reload. (j) **One saved solo run** at a time: a reload restores the Streak, Daily or Speed run started last; starting a Speed Round never wipes a saved Streak run. (k) **Entry:** one-tap button in `.mode-extras` behind `speed_enabled` (missing row = off), a 3-line rules card on the first run only, then a 3-2-1 countdown (later runs and Play again: countdown only). (l) **Result:** big score, personal best line, Play again as the primary button, Share. (m) All numbers in one constants block, "playtest and tune": the owner may adjust them after the local test. **Developer choices 2026-11-25 (for the PO):** (n) fastest and average are over the **correct** answers ("–" when none), so a fast wrong tap never counts; (o) the "+N s" shown is the tier's value even when the 60 s cap absorbs part of it; (p) the question language starts Arabic when the question has it (as the Streak) and **the language the player picks stays for the next questions of the run**; (q) an **End run** button (with a frozen-clock confirm) is shown, and Android Back does the same; (r) no Report button and no read-aloud beyond the open question during a run (Report would cover a running clock). **PO decisions at acceptance 2026-11-25** (test report: 82 PASS, 0 FAIL, 6 PENDING for environment reasons): (s) **BUG-59-01, "+N s" at the cap: keep the tier value** (o stands). The float rewards the speed, next to the speed label; the clock itself stops at 60 s, which is what the AC "up to the cap" requires (C8 PASS). Showing the real gain would read "+0.8 s" or "+0 s" for a lightning answer, which looks like a penalty for being fast. If the owner prefers the real gain after the local test (FU-98), it is a small CR on the float text. (t) **n, p, q, r accepted as built**: averages over correct answers only; the chosen language stays for the run; End run with a frozen-clock confirm; no Report button during a run. The Android Back part of q is accepted on the condition that the device check passes (FU-92). (u) **Other developer choices accepted**: a picture that has not loaded after 5 s starts its question anyway (`SPEED_IMAGE_WAIT_MS`); a mid-run batch failure freezes the clock and shows "Try again"; in the Android app, Back on the rules card or the countdown leaves the app (the run has not started, so nothing is lost); a reload during the countdown restarts the countdown, or the rules card if it was never dismissed; the time left is saved every second while a question is live (`SPEED_SAVE_MS`, BUG-59-03 fix), so a crash gives back at most about 1 s; the 3-2-1 countdown is `aria-live="polite"` (BUG-59-04). (v) **Code-only rows accepted**: 14 PASS rows rest on code review (C6 tier boundaries, R4 "matched your best", F6, F8, E9 and others), and M1 is a partial run because the UI cannot start a Speed Round while a Streak run is live. M3 is partial but the board flow is unchanged. (w) **The Streak failure lines (`streak.nextFailed`, `streak.nextFailedChance`)** have the same missing sentence break that BUG-59-02 fixed for Speed. This is outside US-59 because the Streak code is on `main` and was not part of this story. It is a separate small change (FU-97) and is not bundled into the tested `speed-round` branch. (x) The 320 px header-nav overlap reported by `verify_overflow` predates this story and is outside US-59 (FU-99). **Owner change at the local test 2026-11-25:** (y) the result shows **no fastest-answer or average line**, and Share sends only the score ("Mokh Rush Speed Round: 18 correct"); this replaces the fastest/average part of (l) and makes (n) moot. (z) **No NEW badge** on the Speed Round button, and a high-voltage emoji before "Speed Round" instead of the inline SVG bolt (the same pattern as the Daily buttons' emoji); this replaces the badge part of (k). (aa) **The clock starts at 45 s** (was 30 s; `SPEED_START_S` = 45), owner change at the local test 2026-11-25. (ad) **The seconds shake under 10 s** (owner request at the local test 2026-11-25): while the clock is red (below `SPEED_LOW_S`), the number shakes continuously (`speed-shake`, CSS only, Speed Round only; off under reduced motion). (ac) **No played-questions list in Speed Round** (owner change at the local test 2026-11-25): a run asks for questions without the device's seen list, so they are drawn at random from all eligible questions, and answers are not added to that list (Speed Round no longer moves the "% played" bar or pushes questions to the back for the board and the Streak). The only rule kept: **a question never repeats within one run** (the run's played and queued ids go as `exclude`, and the client drops any duplicate). (ab) **Race-start beeps on the countdown** (owner request at the local test 2026-11-25): a short low beep on 3, 2 and 1 and a long high beep as the run starts, made with Web Audio (no sound file, quiet, no volume control; silent where the browser blocks audio, e.g. a countdown restarted by a reload before any tap, or a phone on silent). US-59. |
| D-74 | Daily Challenge on/off in Admin | **Owner request 2026-11-25** (built on branch `speed-round` together with US-59): Admin > Game settings has a **Daily Challenge** checkbox (`daily_enabled`, 0/1). (a) **A missing row means on**, so the live site keeps showing the button after the deploy until the admin unticks it (unlike `crossword_enabled` / `speed_enabled`, which default to off). (b) Off hides the **Daily Challenge** button on the game page (`updateStreakButtons()`), like the Crossword and Speed Round flags. (c) `api/daily.php` is not blocked and a challenge a player already started on their device can still be finished or restored on reload; only new entries through the button stop. (d) No migration. |
| D-75 | Streak questions not added to the played list | **Owner request 2026-11-25.** (a) A Streak Challenge question is **no longer added** to the device's played-questions list (`brainrush.seenQuestions`): it does not move the "% played" bar and does not push the question to the back for later boards, Streak runs or the final round. (b) A Streak run **still sends the list** as `seen`, so it keeps drawing questions not yet played on the board (or in the Daily Challenge) first. (c) **A question never repeats within one run** (unchanged: the run's played and queued ids go as `exclude`). (d) Side effect accepted by the owner request: since Streak answers are not recorded, a new Streak run may show questions from an earlier Streak run. (e) The Integration Quiz never used the list; Speed Round neither uses nor fills it (D-73 ac); the board, Swap, final round and Daily Challenge still fill it. US-33. |
| D-76 | Speed Round leaderboard (US-60) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-25:** (a) **Ranking = correct answers** (`run.score`), the best run per name (case-insensitive), the earliest run wins ties, top 10, "This week" / "All time" (UTC week, D-46) and the rank line after a run, all as the Streak leaderboard. Only runs with 1 or more correct answers are saved. (b) **Name entry in the Speed popup before the timer**: every run asks, prefilled (the last name used in a Streak or Speed run, else the first player), the Streak's validation; Start, then the 3-2-1 countdown; the name is saved with the run (save version 2). (c) Back on the name step = no run. (d) **Reuse, not a copy:** table `speed_scores` with the `streaks` columns (`streak` = correct answers) in `STREAK_TABLES`, `api/speed-scores.php` on `streak_board_request()`, the same public GET/POST and admin-only list/DELETE; accepted limitation as D-37 (answers in the payload, public POST). **Developer choices (to confirm at the local check):** (e) the name step and the first-run rules are **one card** with Back and Start (no separate rules card, so there is one Start); a visible Back button was added because the card had no way out without Android Back. (f) The setup entry is an **icon-only 🏆 button** right of the Speed Round button (aria-label and tooltip "Speed leaderboard"); the two stay together when the row wraps. (g) The result keeps its own screen; the leaderboard opens from its **Leaderboard** button and Back returns to it. (h) **No ad** on the Speed board (D-41 names the Streak board only). (i) The challenger name is remembered on the device (`brainrush.challengerName`, written by Streak and Speed runs, not by the Integration Quiz) and the privacy page says so. (j) The game log line stays without a name. **PO decisions at acceptance 2026-11-26** (test report: 52 PASS, 2 FAIL, 7 PENDING; fix loop 1): (k) **e to j accepted as built**, still open to the owner's change at the local check (FU-100). (l) **A result without a save state** (version 1, or version 2 with no `save`) counts as "not on the board" (BUG-60-01 fix): its personal best is kept, the result shows no rank line, and the Speed board opened from it shows the rows with no headline, no rank line and no highlighted row. Back still returns to the result. The line "Only runs with 1 or more correct answers ..." shows for a score of 0 only. (m) **API wording:** a missing run on DELETE answers the neutral 404 "Run not found" for all three tables (BUG-60-03). The shared 401 "Login required to change content." (`api/db.php`, every admin endpoint) stays as it is. (n) **Accepted limitation:** a reload while the POST is in flight can save the run twice (as the Streak). The board shows the best per name, so the rank does not change; the admin can delete the extra row. (o) **BUG-60-04** (device "New personal best!" next to the leaderboard "Your best is still N.") goes to the owner as FU-105, with a PO recommendation. (p) **Pending rows** covered elsewhere: N10, R8, R13 and V4 by the developer's browser run (dev note), R9 by code (`dom.speedAgain` / `dom.speedNewGame` disabled while `speedSaving`), B7 by the developer's integration-quiz check and A13; A14 goes to the hosted smoke check (FU-101). |
| D-77 | Tournament mode (US-61) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-26:** (a) **3 to 8 contestants**; empty bracket spots are **byes** (a bye goes straight to the next round). (b) **Each match is a fresh short board** for the two contestants, with the categories and timer chosen in setup and fewer point-value rows (e.g. 3); the higher score wins, a tie goes to Golden Goal. (c) **Finished tournaments are saved on this device only** (`localStorage`), with a "Past tournaments" list that opens the results screen; no server, no migration. (d) **Random draw** with a Shuffle button before the start; **no 3rd-place match**: the two semi-final losers share 3rd. **PO choices 2026-11-26 (the owner can change any of them by a small CR):** (e) **Rows:** the lowest **3 enabled** point values (`TOURNAMENT_ROWS` = 3; live with 600 disabled gives 100, 200, 300), cut in the browser from the normal board payload before the Kilo Meshakkal column and the double cell are dealt; with fewer than 3 enabled, all enabled values. Read at each match start. (f) **Tie-break:** a level match always goes to Golden Goal, **even when `final_round_enabled` is off**, with no "Skip to results" and no Back exit to a tie (overrides D-51 d, e and D-52 a, b inside a tournament); bet cap = score or the largest value on the match board (D-48, D-49 b); repeat rounds until one leads; with no question left, a **random pick** ("Coin toss: <name> goes through"). (g) **Byes:** bracket of 4 for 3–4 entries, of 8 for 5–8; byes take the second slot of first-round matches in the fixed order 1 (bracket of 4) or 1, 4, 2 (bracket of 8), so two byes never meet and they spread over both halves; the shuffle only decides who sits where. (h) **Standings:** 1st, 2nd, joint 3rd (semi-final losers), joint 5th (quarter-final losers), competition ranking (1, 2, 3, 3, 5 …), **plain shared** (scores from different boards are not comparable), listed in bracket order; byes are not losses, so a place can hold fewer entries (3 entries: one 3rd). (i) **Per-match reset:** scores 0, 3 fresh helpers each (Swap and Skip included), a new double cell; a Swap may bring a value not on the short board and is scored at it (D-36 d). (j) **Entries:** a third mode radio **Tournament** (limit 8, "Add contestant", min 3 as a click-time field error, D-07 when switching to Players/Teams with too many); the palette grows to **8** colours in Tournament mode only (2 new colours, AA with the `#0b1026` ink, not grey or gold); colours stay with the entry for the whole tournament. (k) **Flow:** Start → bracket screen (Shuffle until the first match, Start match, End tournament) → match on the existing board and question screens → match result view (Continue, no Share) → bracket with the winner moved on → after the final, tournament results (champion, standings, every match score, Share as US-46, New game). End Game inside a match ends the match (D-09 confirm), not the tournament. (l) **Back and reload:** Android Back on the bracket asks as End tournament; in a match as a normal game; on results goes to setup; a reload restores any tournament screen (D-04, D-25; save shape per D-28). End tournament (confirm) discards the tournament without saving it. (m) **Game log:** one line per tournament at Start, mode `tournament` with all names and the categories; `api/game-log.php` gets `tournament` => 8 (3 to 8 names, else 400); matches are not logged. (n) **History:** `brainrush.tournaments`, newest first, at most **20** (the oldest dropped), each deletable with a confirm (D-18); New game (D-15) does not clear it. (o) **Solo buttons** unchanged (Daily, Crossword, Speed show in any mode, D-47 e; Streak hidden as in Teams). **Repeats:** the played list is sent as `seen` for each match board, so unseen questions come first; no `exclude` on the board API, so a small bank may repeat across matches (accepted). (p) **Surprise me** is drawn once at Start; the same columns serve every match. (q) **Names on the device:** finished tournaments keep the contestant names locally; `privacy.html` says so. (r) **Text:** English plus AI-drafted Arabic in `tournament.*` (D-58 a, D-60; native review FU-107); `how-to-play.html` (#tournament), `llms.txt`, `about.html`, README and `docs/spec.md` updated. **PO decisions at acceptance 2026-11-26** (dev note `docs/sprints/us-61-dev.md`; test report `docs/sprints/us-61-test-report.md`: 66 PASS, 0 FAIL, 4 PENDING, observations O1 to O3): (s) **Developer deviations accepted:** the running tournament is one value under its own key `brainrush.tournament` (`version: 1`, the match board inside it) instead of extending `brainrush.game`; `brainrush.game` stays at version 4, unchanged, so this meets D-28 (a new key with its own version). The open Past list (or Past row) is restored on reload through `sessionStorage` (`brainrush.tournamentPastView`); a new visit starts on setup. After the **final**, the match result shows first, as for any match, and its Continue opens the tournament results (this is the reading of (k)). Back on a match's Golden Goal question asks first: that is what (f) and D-43 a require, not a deviation. (t) **Developer choices accepted** (the owner can change any of them at FU-108): colours pink `#f06bb5` and lime `#a3d13c` (Arabic وردي, ليموني); after End tournament or New game, setup opens in **Players** mode (D-15 empty setup; the Past tournaments button shows once Tournament is picked); the share text lists every place, 5th included; a Past row shows the **local finish** date and time; End Game keeps its label and the normal D-09 confirm inside a match; the Golden Goal offer keeps its normal lead text ("tied for first ..."); the tournament-start log uses the resolved column ids (Surprise already drawn). (u) **O1, byes:** "two byes never meet" means **never in round 1**. With 5 entries (3 byes over 4 quarter-finals) two bye entrants are always in the same half, so they meet in a semi-final whatever the order; 1, 4, 2 already spreads them best (QF1 and QF2 top, QF4 bottom; with 6 entries QF1 and QF4 sit in different halves). Accepted; no change to the bye order. (v) **O2, Start-match error text:** user-safe. The board API answers fixed validation texts (`bad_request` in `api/questions.php`, with category names shown in the page language by `localCategoryNames`), and any uncaught error is a generic "Internal server error" (`api/db.php`; details only in the server log). The reason stays English in the Arabic interface, as on the normal Start (`load.failed`); not a regression. **Accepted limitation:** if the admin makes a picked category unplayable during a tournament (e.g. enables a point value it has no question for), every Start match fails with that 400 and End tournament is the way out; this follows from (e) "read at each match start". (w) **O3:** setup with 8 entries scrolls at 1366x768, as the baseline setup with 6 names does; D-30 covers the game screens, not setup. Accepted. (x) **PENDING rows:** G2 (native Arabic) goes to FU-107; G10 (Tab walk, focus ring) and X6 (Streak, Daily, Speed play) go to the owner's check FU-108, X6 because `saveState()` and the `init()` restore order changed; S10 is covered by code (`updateStreakButtons()`: the Daily, Crossword and Speed buttons do not depend on the mode). (y) **For the record, local data:** the developer's test matches sent 11 "correct" outcomes to the **local** `question_stats` (AI, category 14, 100-point questions). The tester restored the database to the state it found, which already held those 11 counts, so they **remain locally**; the tester's own changes were reverted. Live is not affected (copies go live to local only). No action; at most the local difficulty band of those questions is skewed. **Owner change at the local check 2026-11-26:** (z) **Past tournaments removed** ("remove past tournaments"): no Past tournaments button, list, row view or delete, no `brainrush.tournaments` key and no `sessionStorage` view. This replaces the list part of (c), all of (n), the Past parts of (s) and (t), and narrows (q): only the running tournament (with its names) is kept on the device, until New game or End tournament. The final standings still show on the tournament results screen, which a reload keeps until New game. (aa) **Tournament board point values set in Admin** (owner, 2026-11-26: "make the tournament board dynamic instead of point values from 100 to 300; add in the admin a section for the allowed point values in tournament board"): Admin has a **Tournament board: point values** group, one checkbox per point value, saved as the `tournament_points` setting (ascending comma list; "" or a missing row = none ticked; no migration). *(Owner, same day: the group sits under the Point values (board rows) list with its own **Save tournament board** button, not in Game settings.)* Each match keeps the ticked values that are enabled at its start, any number of them (one ticked value gives a one-row board; all ticked gives the full board); with none ticked or none of them enabled, the lowest 3 enabled values as (e). A disabled value can stay ticked and is used again once enabled. The bet cap of a match Golden Goal follows the match board's largest value, as (f). If the settings cannot be read at Start match, the default rows are used. This replaces the fixed "lowest 3" of (e). (ab) **Categories chosen before each match** (owner, 2026-11-26: "when user choose tournament, disable categories selection and show that categories will be chosen inside the tournament; when the user clicks Start match, pop up categories and let the user choose up to 3 categories only"): in Tournament mode the setup categories are switched off with a note, and Start needs none. **Start match** opens a popup with the playable categories (read when it opens), Surprise me and Kilo Meshakkal; **1 to 3** per match (`TOURNAMENT_PICK_MAX` = 3, a flat cap, not tied to `categories_per_game`); Surprise me as on setup (alone: 3 random; with picks: 1 mystery), drawn for that match. The popup starts with the last match's ticks (memory only; a reload starts empty). The tournament save no longer holds columns. The game log line at Start has no categories ("categories: chosen per match", `api/game-log.php`). This replaces the setup-categories part of (b), all of (p), the categories part of (m) and the "resolved column ids" part of (t); the limitation in (v) now hits only a match whose picked category became unplayable, and the host can pick others. US-61. |
| D-78 | Mokh Bot in Tournament mode (US-62) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-26:** (a) **One weak bot**, "Mokh Bot 🤖", placed at random in **one** of the bye slots of the draw (D-77 g); Shuffle draws it again; no setup option. 4 or 8 humans: no bot; 3: it fills the only bye; 5: one of 3; 6: one of 2; 7: the only one. The bracket size and the bye slots still come from the human count. (b) **The bot advances** when it wins; it can win the tournament and appears in the standings and the share text. (c) **A match with the bot is turn-based**: the two sides alternate, a wrong answer loses the points and the turn passes, **no steal**. Human v human matches are unchanged. (d) **First turn: a coin flip** with an animation at the start of the bot match. (e) **Name with the emoji**, "Mokh Bot 🤖" / "مخ بوت 🤖": an **owner exception to the no-emoji rule** (the robot emoji in this one name only). The `check_no_emoji` gate has no allow-list setting, so the source files (`js/`, `how-to-play.html`) write the emoji as an escape and stay emoji-free, the docs carry the literal robot under this exception, and this decision records it. *(Clarified at PO acceptance, see (v); the owner confirms the escape instead of the allow-list at FU-111.)* **From the approved plan 2026-11-26:** (f) bot accuracy about 0.35 on the lowest row down to about 0.20 on the highest (`BOT_ACCURACY`) *(superseded by D-79)*; (g) on its turn the bot picks a cell after about 1.2 s, answers 3 to 6 s after its question opens (always before the timer ends) and is scored about 1.5 s later; (h) a wrong roll says a stored wrong option (cut as the 3 choices helper) or "I don't know…" when the question has none; (i) Golden Goal: the bot locks its bet (0 to half its D-48 limit, in hundreds) when its turn comes and marks itself *(the bet rule superseded by D-79 h)*; (j) the save stays `TOURNAMENT_VERSION` 1: `bot` on the entry and `turn`, `coinShown` and `botRoll` on the match board are optional (D-56 a); (k) the game log line holds the human names only. **Developer choices 2026-11-26 (for the PO; dev note `docs/sprints/us-62-dev.md`):** (l) Back to Board is disabled on the bot's question until the bot is scored (at most about 7.5 s), so its cell never closes unanswered; (m) the Pause button stops the countdown only, not the bot; (n) when the bot opens the double score cell, the popup waits for the host's "Let's play" and the bot starts thinking after it; (o) accuracy 0.25 on a Golden Goal question (the plan gives no number); the bet is a random whole amount from 0 to half the limit (rounded down), then rounded to the **nearest** hundred and never above the limit, so a 200 limit gives 0 or 100 (fix loop 1, BUG-62-02) *(superseded by D-79 g and h)*; (p) the bot's own board questions send **no** outcome to the question stats (US-47), since its answers are dice rolls (cf. D-77 y); questions on the human's turn in a bot match send theirs as usual; (q) the bot's scoreboard card has no helper chips; a full board hides the turn banner; (r) a reload during the coin flip plays it again (`coinShown` is saved when the flip ends); a reload during "Thinking…" starts the wait again with the same saved answer, and a reload once the answer shows shows it at once and scores it about 1.5 s later (`botRoll.said`, fix loop 1, OBS-2); "Thinking…" shows only once the double score popup is closed (BUG-62-03); the waiting side is greyed by its row (panel background, dashed border, muted score), never by dimming the name chip, so the name keeps AA contrast (BUG-62-01); (s) the bracket info line counts the bot as an entry; (t) one extra string, `helper.notBot` (the tooltip reason on the bot's hidden helper chips). **PO decisions at acceptance 2026-11-26** (test report `docs/sprints/us-62-test-report.md`: first run 81 PASS, 3 FAIL, 5 PENDING; re-test after fix loop 1: 19 PASS, 1 FAIL; BUG-62-01 to 03 fixed and verified): (u) **Developer choices l to t confirmed as built**, the owner can change any of them at the local check (FU-111). l keeps the bot's cell from closing unanswered; m is fine because the bot always answers before the timer ends (g); n treats the bot like any contestant on the double cell; o (Golden Goal accuracy 0.25, bet 0 to half the limit rounded to the nearest hundred, capped) is confirmed, a 200 limit giving 0 or 100 is what (i) means; p keeps dice rolls out of the difficulty stats; q, r, s and t need no change. (v) **Emoji exception, scope made exact (OBS-1):** the plan's allow-list step was replaced because the gate has no allow-list. The exception covers **only the robot in the bot's name**: in source it is written as an escape (`\u{1F916}` in both tables of `js/i18n.*.js`, `String.fromCodePoint(0x1f916)` for the coin face in `js/game.*.js`, `&#129302;` in `how-to-play.html`); the docs (`docs/product-backlog.md`, `docs/spec.md`) carry the literal robot under the same exception. The gate passes because it does not decode escapes; that is **not** a general way around the no-emoji rule: any other emoji, escaped or literal, still needs its own owner decision. (w) **BUG-62-04 accepted as is, no follow-up:** the 5 remaining `lint_hardcodes` findings are animation durations in the US-62 CSS block (`0.7s` pick highlight, `1.2s` Thinking pulse, `1.6s` twice for the coin spin, equal to `COIN_SPIN_MS`, `0.3s` coin fade, equal to `leaveCoin`); the project has no duration tokens and the stylesheet already had 238 findings on `main`, so a token for 5 values in one story is not worth it. The colours, radii and lengths of the block use tokens or rem. (x) **OBS-3 accepted:** the coin overlay scrolls 5 px vertically at 320 px; the 320 px rule (D-30) is about sideways scroll. (y) **Pending rows** go to the owner's check FU-111: Android Back during the coin flip (row 56, code only so far) and a short Tab walk on the bot's turn (row 55); native Arabic stays FU-110; `validate_contrast` (row 11) is replaced by the measured contrast (rows 79 to 82, R2, R3, R8). US-62. |
| D-79 | Mokh Bot accuracy meter and smart board play (US-63) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-26 (supersede D-78 f, i and o):** (a) **One accuracy meter** for the bot, set in Admin as a slider, 0% to 100% in steps of 10 (setting `bot_accuracy`, stored as right tickets 0 to 10). (b) **10 tickets:** at A% there are A/10 right tickets; the rest are split, the larger half wrong and the smaller half "I don't know" (30%: 3, 4, 3; 70%: 7, 2, 1). (c) **Fixed layout** on the dice faces, right, then wrong, then "I don't know"; the dice is fair, so the order does not change the odds. (d) A right face says the real answer (Correct); a wrong face says that wrong option confidently; an "I don't know" face says "I don't know…" and never a wrong option. Both wrong and "I don't know" are scored Wrong and lose the points. (e) A fresh roll for every question. (f) A question stores only 2 wrong options, so the wrong faces alternate `wrong_1`, `wrong_2`; with none stored a wrong face says "I don't know…". (g) **The meter is flat:** no row slope; Golden Goal uses the same dice (no more 0.25). (h) **Golden Goal bet** = accuracy x the bet limit, rounded to the nearest 100, never above the limit (1000 at 70%: 700; 200 at 30%: 100, reachable only with just the 200 row enabled; on the default board the smallest limit is 600, giving 200); no randomness. (i) **Smart board play:** with p = accuracy, above 50% the bot takes the highest open value; at 50% or less the highest when it is behind and the lowest when it is ahead or level, re-checked before every pick; then a random category among the open cells of that value; it never uses the hidden double cell (D-40). **From the approved plan:** a missing setting row means 30% (close to the old level, so the live site does not change until the admin moves the slider); the value is read once per match with the match's settings and saved on the match board as the optional `botTickets` (D-56 a, `TOURNAMENT_VERSION` stays 1; an older save plays at 30%); no migration (the settings save already upserts). **Developer choices 2026-11-27 (for the PO; dev note `docs/sprints/us-63-dev.md`):** (j) the Kilo Meshakkal boxes hide their value (US-53), so reading it would be cheating like the double cell; the bot chooses among the cells whose value shows and opens a Kilo Meshakkal box, at random, only when no other cell is open; (k) the Admin slider sits under "Tournament board" in the Point values panel, in its own box with its own Save button, like the tournament board; it applies to matches started after the save; (l) a Save of the main Game settings form reloads the slider from the saved value, so an unsaved slider move is reset (the same as the tournament board ticks). **PO acceptance 2026-11-27 (test report `docs/sprints/us-63-test-report.md`):** (m) j, k and l are accepted; the owner confirms them at the local check (FU-115), else names a small CR. (n) BUG-63-01 (Admin sideways scroll at 320 px, pre-existing on main) is not part of US-63; logged as FU-114. (o) `PUT settings.php` accepts a numeric string such as `"7"` as 7, like every integer setting (shared `parse_int`); non-numeric text is 400. No change. (p) The bot's spoken answer follows the question's language and "Mokh Bot says:" / "I don't know…" follow the interface language, the same rule as for humans (OBS-63-02); the owner confirms at FU-115. US-63. |
| D-80 | Phone buzzer game control (US-52, US-64, US-65) | **Owner decisions 2026-11-27 (approved plan; replace the 2026-11-02 buzz-button design of US-52):** (a) **Game control** choice on setup, under the categories: **Host control** = today's game, unchanged (Golden Goal included); **Phone buzzer** = players use their own phones. (b) **Players and Teams modes only**; Tournament (with Mokh Bot) is unchanged and does not show the choice. (c) **Joining:** the host shares a link, QR code and room code; a phone picks its own name from the names the host typed (no accounts, no typing); a claimed name is greyed out, so **one device per name** (per team in Teams mode, the holder answers for the team); a private device token makes a refreshed phone **rejoin as the same name**; the host lobby shows joined / waiting and a **Release** button frees a name. (d) **Board:** read-only and live on phones; **only the host picks cells**; the host screen stays the source of truth for board and scores. (e) **Questions:** when the host opens a cell, every phone shows the question and a text box; **one answer per contestant, the first sent is locked**; answers are accepted from the open until the timer ends or the host shows the answer, **late answers are rejected**; the answer is **never sent to phones**. (f) The host screen lists the answers **in arrival order** (name, answer, time) and the **host still judges** with Correct / Wrong. (g) Helpers stay on the host; with **3 choices** the options also show on phones as buttons that fill the answer. (h) **Golden Goal in Phone buzzer:** private bets on phones while the host shows "Waiting for players to bet (n/N)", then the same phone-answer rule, results on the host; Golden Goal under Host control unchanged. (i) **Tech:** Hostinger shared hosting, no WebSockets and no cron: short HTTP polling about every 1 s; rooms idle over **6 hours** are deleted when a request comes in; FU-24 (spike) **cancelled**, replaced by a latency check in phone Sprint 1 (delay and requests per minute with 1, 5 and 10 tabs). Delivered in phone Sprints 0 to 3 on branch `phone-buzzer`, one PR per sprint, replacing the old Sprint 14 plan; EN + AR with right-to-left (D-60). *(The plan file names this decision D-79; D-79 was already taken by US-63, so it is D-80.)* **PO choices 2026-11-27 (the owner can change any of them by a small CR):** (j) **Bet range:** the plan says "between 0 and the player's score"; read literally, a contestant at 0 or below could only bet 0. The PO reads it as the **D-48 bet limit** (score or the largest enabled point value, whichever is higher), the same as Host control; owner to confirm (FU-117). (k) **Missing bets:** the host can tap "Continue without missing bets"; a missing bet counts as 0, so a contestant with no device never blocks the round. (l) **Security:** the link and QR code hold only the room code, never a token; answer text is at most 200 characters and shown as text, never HTML (US-27); prepared statements only (US-26). (m) **Lateness and order:** the server accepts answers only while the question number is open (between the host's open and close calls; the host closes at timer 0, answer shown, Skip, Swap or Back, so a Pause keeps it open); arrival order uses server time, never the phone's clock. (n) **No one joined:** Phone buzzer mode still plays; the host scores by hand. (o) **Priority:** US-52, US-64 and US-65 are Should (US-52 was Could), as the owner committed them to sprints. (p) **No leaks to phones:** the phone payload hides what the host board hides: Kilo Meshakkal boxes show their emoji with no value (US-53) and the double cell position (D-40) is not sent until it is opened. **PO acceptance of phone Sprint 1, 2026-11-27 (US-52 ACCEPTED with follow-ups;** test report `docs/sprints/phone-sprint-1-test.md`: first run 27 PASS, 2 FAIL, 9 PENDING; Re-test 1: BUG-52-01 to 03 fixed and passed; BUG-52-04 fixed in fix round 2, dev suite 47/47, fix checked in code by the PO; dev note `docs/sprints/phone-sprint-1-dev.md`). The owner can change any of q to v by a small CR at the local check (FU-118): (q) **Two host tabs: the newest tab wins.** A tab that loads the saved game claims the room; the older tab stops pushing, polling and saving and shows "Open in another tab" with **Use this tab** (reload and take over). Accepted. Known edge accepted: a change made in the older tab in the up-to-2 s window before its notice shows is lost from that tab's save; the phones are never overwritten. (r) **The lobby call is public:** anyone with the room code sees the names the host typed, their colours and joined / waiting; the board and scores need the device token. Accepted: the code is shared openly in the room, the names are the host's own entries, and `privacy.html` ("Playing with phones") says what is stored. (s) **New Game leaves the old room to expire** after 6 h idle; its phones keep the last board with "Game over.". Accepted for phone Sprint 1; closing the room and the "game ended" phone screen stay in phone Sprint 3 (US-65). (t) **Room creation is public with no rate limit,** like the game log. Accepted: a body is at most 256 KB (413), the stored state at most 128 KB, one room per game start, rooms deleted after 6 h idle (the dev note's "32 KB" in limitation 2 is not the cap; the API caps are these). Revisit if abuse shows in the hosting panel, or at phone Sprint 3 (OBS-52-01). (u) **The service worker caches `join.html` and its files when they load** (network first, `brainrush-join-v1`), not as an install precache, because the hashed names change on every commit; `api/` is never cached. Accepted: it meets the criterion; a phone that never loaded the join page has no offline copy, and it needs the network to join anyway. (v) **Other developer choices confirmed:** a failed room creation keeps setup with the message and every input (no Phone buzzer game without a room; Host control is the fallback); the ×2 mark reaches phones only when the double cell is opened (as p); Game control goes back to Host control on New Game (per game). (w) **Golden Goal "Show results" (Re-test 1): not a bug.** The same steps behave the same on `main` (fix round 2): equal bets with both wrong give **Next round** (D-51), otherwise **See results** is enabled once all are marked. No change. (x) **PENDING rows:** 3b closed by the fix round 2 runs on the branch and on `main` (double cell and full Golden Goal under Host control, 0 rooms calls); 14 passed in Re-test 1; 25b and 31 closed by the BUG-52-04 fix (measured by the developer, checked in code by the PO; the owner sees it in FU-118); 28b did not recur; 9b (Teams with two real phones), 29b (two-tab 2 s window) and 4b (real QR scan) go to FU-118; 16 and 26 (about 1 s; live latency with 1, 5 and 10 tabs) go to FU-120; native Arabic review to FU-121. (y) **Latency criterion: accepted pending FU-120,** since it can only be measured on the live host. If the live delay is well over 1 s or the host shows errors, the PO and owner decide before phone Sprint 2. Phone Sprints 2 and 3 are **deferred by the owner (2026-11-27) until they ask**. US-52, US-64, US-65. |

(3) **Phone buzzer, Players, 3 names:** Start; the panel shows the QR code, the room code, the link and Copy link, all 3 names Waiting; Close and reopen it with **Phones 0/3**. (4) Each "phone" needs its **own browser storage** (tabs and windows of one profile share one device token and rejoin as the same name): host in normal Chrome, phone 1 in an Incognito window (DevTools device toolbar 375x812), phone 2 in Edge or another Chrome profile. Phone 1 picks a name; within about 2 s the host shows Joined and Phones 1/3. On phone 2 that name is greyed out. Start a Phone buzzer game and **scan the QR code** with a phone on the same Wi-Fi: it opens the join page; pick a name and follow a played cell; refresh the phone (same name). Plain http at a LAN address is not a secure context for the host page or the phone: neither page registers its service worker and **Copy link** on the host may fail.
